//! A process-local clock for development and explicitly classified staging stores.
use snapback4_core::error::Refusal;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

const MAX_TIME: i64 = snapback4_core::value::I53_MAX;

pub fn input(message: impl Into<String>) -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INPUT", "clock", message).guide("clock") }
pub fn release() -> Refusal { Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY", "clock", "clock controls require dev or a deploy --staging store; production always uses wall time").guide("clock") }

/// Isolated time tests stay inside an assigned range, or request a free port.
pub(crate) fn isolated_command() -> String {
    isolated_range(std::env::var("PORT_LO").ok().as_deref(), std::env::var("PORT_HI").ok().as_deref())
}
fn isolated_range(lo: Option<&str>, hi: Option<&str>) -> String {
    match (lo.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok()), hi.and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())) {
        (Some(lo), Some(hi)) if lo > 0 && hi >= lo => format!("dev --memory --port {lo}-{hi} within the current range"),
        _ => "dev --memory --port 0".into(),
    }
}

pub fn duration(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    if text.starts_with('-') { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
    let text = text.strip_prefix('+').unwrap_or(text);
    let split = text.bytes().position(|b| !b.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(text.len());
    let (number, unit) = text.split_at(split);
    let multiplier = match unit { "ms" => 1, "s" => 1_000, "m" => 60_000, "h" => 3_600_000, "d" => 86_400_000, "w" => 604_800_000, _ => 0 };
    number.parse::<i64>().ok().and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(multiplier)).filter(|n| *n > 0 && *n <= MAX_TIME)
        .ok_or_else(|| input("expected a positive whole duration with ms, s, m, h, d or w, such as +25h"))
}

pub fn offset(text: &str) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    if let Some(rest) = text.strip_prefix('-') { duration(rest).map(|n| -n) } else { duration(text) }
}

fn time(value: Option<i64>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    value.filter(|n| (0..=MAX_TIME).contains(n)).ok_or_else(|| input("time must be Unix milliseconds in 0..9007199254740991; choose a smaller time or duration"))
}

pub fn run_now(text: Option<&String>) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
    let now = snapback4_core::now_ms();
    match text {
        None => Ok(now),
        Some(text) if text.starts_with('+') => time(now.checked_add(duration(text)?)),
        Some(text) => time(text.parse().ok()),
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct Clock {
    dev: bool,
    base: i64,
    offset: i64,
    started: Instant,
    epoch: String,
    revision: u64,
    #[cfg(test)]
    frozen: bool,
}

impl Clock {
    pub fn new(dev: bool, offset: i64) -> Result<Self, Refusal> {
        if !dev && offset != 0 { return Err(release()); }
        Ok(Self { dev, offset, base: time(snapback4_core::now_ms().checked_add(offset))?, started: Instant::now(),
            epoch: snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?, revision: 0, #[cfg(test)] frozen: false })
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn freeze(&mut self, now: i64) { self.base = now; self.frozen = true; }
    pub fn now(&self) -> i64 {
        #[cfg(test)] if self.frozen { return self.base; }
        if !self.dev { return snapback4_core::now_ms(); }
        self.base.saturating_add(self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64).min(MAX_TIME)
    }
    /// Identifies samples taken before/after a restart or deliberate clock jump.
    pub fn offset(&self) -> i64 { self.offset }
    pub fn revision(&self) -> String { format!("{}:{}", self.epoch, self.revision) }
    pub fn presence_now(&self) -> i64 {
        // Production grace is elapsed real time, unaffected by wall-clock corrections.
        if self.dev { self.now() } else { self.started.elapsed().as_millis().min(MAX_TIME as u128) as i64 }
    }
    /// Back to wall time: the offset `--clock-offset` and every advance added
    /// is dropped (a development reset's `{"clock":"reset"}`).
    pub fn reset(&mut self) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
        self.revision += 1;
        self.offset = 0;
        self.base = snapback4_core::now_ms();
        self.started = Instant::now();
        #[cfg(test)] { self.frozen = false; }
        Ok(self.base)
    }
    pub fn advance(&mut self, milliseconds: i64) -> Result<i64, Refusal> {
        if !self.dev { return Err(release()); }
        if milliseconds <= 0 { return Err(input("clock advance cannot move backwards; use a positive duration such as +2m")); }
        let next = time(self.now().checked_add(milliseconds))?;
        let offset = self.offset.checked_add(milliseconds).ok_or_else(|| input("cumulative clock offset overflow"))?;
        self.revision += 1;
        self.offset = offset;
        self.base = next;
        self.started = Instant::now();
        Ok(next)
    }
}

/// The control CLI only talks to a loopback dev host. It does not open its store.
pub fn command(options: &crate::verbs::Options) -> i32 {
    let result = (|| -> Result<String, String> {
        if options.positionals.len() != 2 || options.positionals[0] != "advance" {
            return Err(input(format!("usage: {}", crate::cli_flags::synopsis("clock"))).to_string());
        }
        duration(&options.positionals[1]).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        let release_path = options.flags.get("release").map(|dir| std::path::PathBuf::from(dir).join("store.sqlite"));
        if release_path.is_some() && options.flags.contains_key("port") { return Err("clock --release does not accept --port".into()); }
        let (address, instance, token) = if let Some(path) = &release_path {
            let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(path).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?;
            if store.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(release().to_string()); }
            let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(path).ok_or("staging clock requires a running serve")?;
            let session = crate::serve::token::mint_live(path, "clock", 1).map_err(|(_,error)|error)?;
            (address,None,Some(session.token))
        } else { let (address,instance)=crate::verbs::dev_address(options)?; (address,instance,None) };
        let body = serde_json::json!({"duration":options.positionals[1],"eachBoundary":options.flags.contains_key("each-boundary")}).to_string();
        // The common HTTP/1.0 exchange is EOF-delimited and bounds replies at
        // 64 MiB, enough for the preflight's complete explicit window report.
        let response = if let Some(token) = &token {
            crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_authorized(address,"POST","/dev/clock/advance",None,Some(token),&body,Duration::from_secs(60))
        } else { crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange_dev(address, "/dev/clock/advance", instance.as_deref(), &body, Duration::from_secs(60)) };
        let (status, json) = response.map_err(|error| {
            let evidence = crate::verbs::run_forward::exchange(address, "GET", "/dev/status", None, "", Duration::from_secs(5)).ok()
                .map(|(_, status)| format!("now={}, skewMs={}", status["now"], status["skewMs"]))
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "clock status unavailable".into());
            format!("clock advance reply failed at {address}: {error}; time may already have advanced ({evidence}); inspect /dev/status and queued work before retrying; for an isolated dev pass --port N")
        })?;
        if let Some(denied) = json.get("denied") {
            let clock = json["message"].as_str().map(|message| format!("; time remains advanced: {message}")).unwrap_or_default();
            return Err(format!("{}: {}{clock}\n  guide: clock", denied["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("E_CLOCK_INPUT"), denied["message"].as_str().unwrap_or("clock advance failed")));
        }
        if status != 200 { return Err(format!("dev refused clock advance: {json}")); }
        Ok(json.to_string())
    })();
    match result { Ok(body) => { println!("{body}"); 0 }, Err(error) => { eprintln!("{error}"); 1 } }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod isolated_tests {
    #[test]
    fn time_test_guidance_uses_only_valid_assigned_ranges_or_a_free_port() {
        assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(Some("24000"), Some("24009")), "dev --memory --port 24000-24009 within the current range");
        for (lo,hi) in [(None,None),(Some("0"),Some("3")),(Some("4"),Some("3")),(Some("bad"),Some("3"))] {
            assert_eq!(super::isolated_range(lo,hi), "dev --memory --port 0");
        }
    }
}
//! Bounded, read-only evidence from the store's own commit clock.
use snapback4_core::{Engine, RuntimeError};
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

const SKIPPED_PREVIEW: usize = 32;
const TOLERANCE_MS: i64 = 5_000;
const RECENT_COMMITS: usize = 64;
const TABLE_BYTES: usize = 4096;
const TABLE_NAMES: usize = 8;
const REPORT_ERROR_BYTES: usize = 1024;
// Reserve one MiB for the outer clock/work envelope inside the host encoder.
const REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET: usize = crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING - (1 << 20);

// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — inspect a fixed recent commit window, never application row values.
pub fn ahead(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Result<Option<String>, RuntimeError> {
    // Reverse primary-key traversal: no full scan, sort, row payloads or retained
    // historical stamp set. octet_length(column) uses SQLite record metadata;
    // unlike a text length/cast/substr, it need not load oversized payloads.
    let mut query = engine.store().conn().prepare(
        "SELECT at, CASE WHEN at > ?1 AND octet_length(touched) <= ?2 THEN CAST(touched AS BLOB) END,
         octet_length(touched) FROM _commits ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT ?3")?;
    let mut rows = query.query(rusqlite::params![now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS), TABLE_BYTES as i64, RECENT_COMMITS as i64])?;
    let mut lead = 0;
    let mut tables = BTreeSet::new();
    let mut omitted = false;
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        let at: i64 = row.get(0)?;
        if at <= now.saturating_add(TOLERANCE_MS) { continue; }
        lead = lead.max(at.saturating_sub(now));
        let length: i64 = row.get(2)?;
        if length > TABLE_BYTES as i64 { omitted = true; continue; }
        let bytes: Vec<u8> = row.get(1)?;
        match serde_json::from_slice::<Vec<String>>(&bytes) {
            Ok(names) => for name in names {
                if tables.contains(&name) { continue; }
                if tables.len() < TABLE_NAMES && name.len() <= 128 { tables.insert(name); }
                else { omitted = true; }
            },
            Err(_) => omitted = true,
        }
    }
    if lead == 0 { return Ok(None); }
    let mut names = tables.into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if omitted { names.push("additional tables omitted".into()); }
    if names.is_empty() { names.push("no table names recorded".into()); }
    Ok(Some(format!(
        "N_CLOCK_STORE_AHEAD: recent commit stamps lead the current clock by up to {lead}ms (latest {RECENT_COMMITS} commits; tables: {}); use a fresh store per offset test ({}), or restart with the same --clock-offset (or dev --resume-clock) to continue",
        names.join(", "), crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())))
}

/// UTC calendar text for Unix milliseconds: schedule boundaries are UTC.
pub fn utc(ms: i64) -> String {
    let (days, rem) = (ms.div_euclid(86_400_000), ms.rem_euclid(86_400_000));
    // Civil-from-days (proleptic Gregorian), exact for the I53 range.
    let z = days + 719_468;
    let era = z.div_euclid(146_097);
    let doe = z - era * 146_097;
    let yoe = (doe - doe / 1_460 + doe / 36_524 - doe / 146_096) / 365;
    let doy = doe - (365 * yoe + yoe / 4 - yoe / 100);
    let mp = (5 * doy + 2) / 153;
    let day = doy - (153 * mp + 2) / 5 + 1;
    let month = if mp < 10 { mp + 3 } else { mp - 9 };
    let year = yoe + era * 400 + i64::from(month <= 2);
    format!("{year:04}-{month:02}-{day:02}T{:02}:{:02}:{:02}Z", rem / 3_600_000, rem % 3_600_000 / 60_000, rem % 60_000 / 1_000)
}

/// A positive duration in the units `clock advance` accepts, rounded up to a second.
pub fn span(ms: i64) -> String {
    let seconds = (ms.max(0) + 999) / 1_000;
    let (d, h, m, s) = (seconds / 86_400, seconds % 86_400 / 3_600, seconds % 3_600 / 60, seconds % 60);
    let parts: Vec<String> = [(d, "d"), (h, "h"), (m, "m"), (s, "s")].into_iter().filter(|(n, _)| *n > 0).map(|(n, unit)| format!("{n}{unit}")).collect();
    if parts.is_empty() { "0s".into() } else { parts.join("") }
}

/// Each schedule's saved boundary, in declaration order.
pub fn saved_boundaries(engine: &Engine) -> Vec<Option<i64>> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter()
        .map(|schedule| {
            let shape = engine.store().meta(&format!("schedule-shape:{}", schedule.name)).ok().flatten();
            if shape.as_ref().is_some_and(|shape| *shape != snapback4_core::value::digest(schedule)) { return None; }
            engine.store().meta(&format!("schedule:{}", schedule.name)).ok().flatten().and_then(|value| value.parse().ok())
        })
        .collect()
}

/// Preflight is bounded before the clock or worker leases change.
// @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12.1 — only explicit execution enumerates all crossed windows; coalesced reports keep a bounded preview.
pub fn crossed_boundaries(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool) -> Result<Vec<(usize, i64)>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    let mut boundaries = Vec::new();
    let mut report_bytes = 0usize;
    for (index, schedule) in engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate() {
        // JSON may escape every error byte; count that bound per schedule.
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add(2048 + 6 * (REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3) + 8 * (schedule.name.len() + schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule report exceeds its byte budget; reduce the declared schedules")); }
        if held { continue; }
        let Some(old) = before.get(index).copied().flatten() else { continue; };
        let end = schedule.boundary(now);
        if old >= end { continue; }
        let periods = schedule.periods_between(old, end);
        if each_boundary && periods > (10_000 - boundaries.len()) as i64 {
            return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("--each-boundary crosses more than 10000 schedule windows; use a smaller duration or omit --each-boundary to coalesce"));
        }
        report_bytes = report_bytes.saturating_add((if each_boundary { periods as usize } else { (periods as usize).min(SKIPPED_PREVIEW + 1) }).saturating_mul(1024 + 8 * schedule.target.len()));
        if report_bytes > REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET { return Err(crate::dev_clock::input("schedule window report exceeds its byte budget; use a smaller duration")); }
        if !each_boundary { continue; }
        let mut boundary = schedule.next(old);
        while boundary <= end {
            boundaries.push((index, boundary));
            boundary = schedule.next(boundary);
        }
    }
    boundaries.sort_by_key(|(index, at)| (*at, *index));
    Ok(boundaries)
}

fn period(schedule: &snapback4_core::schema::Schedule) -> String {
    schedule.describe()
}

/// What each schedule did on one clock advance, and why one did not fire.
/// The core fires each schedule at most once per advance, for the latest
/// boundary, and holds it while its saved boundary is ahead of the clock.
// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — missed boundaries fire once; this only explains them.
pub fn schedule_report(engine: &Engine, before: &[Option<i64>], fired: &[String], now: i64, held: bool, each_boundary: bool, port: Option<u16>) -> Vec<serde_json::Value> {
    let after = saved_boundaries(engine);
    // Read only bounded summaries: job args/cursors and full error payloads
    // are irrelevant to this report and must not be loaded then replicated.
    let runs = job_summaries(engine).unwrap_or_default();
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().enumerate().map(|(index, schedule)| {
        let (old, saved) = (before.get(index).copied().flatten(), after.get(index).copied().flatten());
        let current = schedule.boundary(now);
        let next = schedule.next(current);
        let mut entry = serde_json::json!({ "name": schedule.name, "target": schedule.target, "period": period(schedule), "fired": false, "firedWindows": [], "skippedBoundaries": [], "nextBoundary": utc(next) });
        if fired.contains(&schedule.name) {
            let end = saved.unwrap_or(current);
            entry["fired"] = true.into();
            let start = schedule.previous(end);
            let window = |boundary| serde_json::json!({"periodStart": schedule.previous(boundary), "periodEnd": boundary});
            let mut windows = Vec::new();
            if each_boundary {
                if let Some(old) = old {
                    let mut at = schedule.next(old);
                    while at <= end { windows.push(window(at)); at = schedule.next(at); }
                }
            } else { windows.push(window(end)); }
            if each_boundary {
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired {} windows: {}", windows.len(), windows.iter().map(|window| format!("{} to {}", utc(window["periodStart"].as_i64().unwrap()), utc(window["periodEnd"].as_i64().unwrap()))).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("; ")).into();
            }
            entry["firedWindows"] = windows.into();
            entry["periodStart"] = start.into();
            entry["periodEnd"] = end.into();
            if !each_boundary { entry["reason"] = format!("fired for the period {} to {}", utc(start), utc(end)).into(); }
            if let Some(run) = runs.get(&schedule.target) { entry["run"] = run.clone(); }
            // @ref LLP 3000.040#24-monthly-and-yearly-schedules — calendar periods count boundaries, not milliseconds.
            let skipped = if each_boundary { 0 } else { old.map(|old| schedule.periods_between(old, end) - 1).unwrap_or(0) };
            if skipped > 0 {
                entry["skippedPeriods"] = skipped.into();
                let mut windows = Vec::new();
                let mut at = schedule.next(old.unwrap());
                while at < end && windows.len() < SKIPPED_PREVIEW {
                    let mut item = window(at);
                    let mut args = serde_json::Map::new();
                    if let Some(program) = engine.program(&schedule.target) {
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodStart") { args.insert("periodStart".into(), schedule.previous(at).into()); }
                        if program.args.contains_key("periodEnd") { args.insert("periodEnd".into(), at.into()); }
                    }
                    item["command"] = format!("snapback4 run {} '{}'{}", schedule.target, serde_json::Value::Object(args), port.map(|port| format!(" --port {port}")).unwrap_or_default()).into();
                    windows.push(item); at = schedule.next(at);
                }
                entry["skippedBoundariesOmitted"] = (skipped - windows.len() as i64).into();
                entry["skippedBoundaries"] = windows.into();
                entry["reason"] = format!("fired once, for the latest period {} to {}; {skipped} earlier period{} this advance crossed (from {}) did not run: missed boundaries fire once. Run {} with periodStart/periodEnd for an earlier period",
                    utc(start), utc(end), if skipped == 1 { "" } else { "s" }, utc(old.unwrap_or(end)), schedule.target).into();
            }
        } else if held {
            entry["reason"] = "schedules are held (dev --hold-schedules or --replay); a restart without the flag resumes at the next boundary".into();
        } else if let Some(saved) = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > current) {
            let due = schedule.next(saved);
            entry["heldUntil"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["nextBoundary"] = utc(due).into();
            entry["reason"] = format!("held by the store: its saved boundary {} is ahead of the clock {} (a dev restart resets the clock offset; the store keeps the boundary). Advance {} more, restart with dev --resume-clock, or test on dev --fresh",
                utc(saved), utc(now), span(due - now)).into();
        } else if old != saved {
            entry["reason"] = format!("{} started at boundary {}: a schedule fires at the boundary after the first one it sees, next {} ({} from now)",
                if old.is_none() { "this schedule" } else { "this changed schedule" }, utc(saved.unwrap_or(current)), utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        } else {
            entry["reason"] = format!("next boundary {} ({} from now; schedule times are UTC)", utc(next), span(next - now)).into();
        }
        entry
    }).collect()
}

/// The report never loads full error/argument payloads from retained jobs.
fn job_summaries(engine: &Engine) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value>, RuntimeError> {
    let mut statement = engine.store().conn().prepare(
        "SELECT name, state, rows_written, substr(error, 1, ?1), octet_length(error) > ?1, id FROM _jobs ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 64")?;
    let mut rows = statement.query([REPORT_ERROR_BYTES as i64])?;
    let mut summaries = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {
        let name: String = row.get(0)?;
        if summaries.contains_key(&name) { continue; }
        let mut error: Option<String> = row.get(3)?;
        let truncated = row.get::<_, Option<bool>>(4)?.unwrap_or(false);
        if let Some(text) = &mut error && truncated {
            let mut end = REPORT_ERROR_BYTES.min(text.len());
            while !text.is_char_boundary(end) { end -= 1; }
            text.truncate(end); text.push('…');
        }
        summaries.insert(name, serde_json::json!({ "id":row.get::<_, String>(5)?, "state":row.get::<_, String>(1)?,
            "rowsWritten":row.get::<_, i64>(2)?, "error":error, "errorTruncated":truncated }));
    }
    Ok(summaries)
}

/// Startup lines for schedules the store holds: their saved boundary leads the clock.
pub fn held_schedules(engine: &Engine, now: i64) -> Vec<String> {
    engine.schema().schedules.iter().zip(saved_boundaries(engine)).filter_map(|(schedule, saved)| {
        let saved = saved.filter(|saved| *saved > schedule.boundary(now))?;
        let due = schedule.next(saved);
        Some(format!("clock     schedule {} is held until {} ({} from now): its saved boundary {} is ahead of this clock; `clock advance {}` or restart with dev --resume-clock",
            schedule.name, utc(due), span(due - now), utc(saved), span(due - now)))
    }).collect()
}

/// The dev clock offset that continues a store's own time: its newest
/// recent commit stamp (or saved schedule boundary) less wall time, never negative.
pub fn resume_offset(store: &snapback4_core::Store, wall: i64) -> Result<i64, RuntimeError> {
    let commit: Option<i64> = store.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT MAX(at) FROM (SELECT at FROM _commits ORDER BY seq DESC LIMIT ?1)", [RECENT_COMMITS as i64], |row| row.get(0))?;
    let mut statement = store.conn().prepare("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key LIKE 'schedule:%'")?;
    let boundary = statement.query_map([], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0))?.flatten().filter_map(|value| value.parse::<i64>().ok()).max();
    let latest = commit.into_iter().chain(boundary).max().unwrap_or(wall);
    // Whole seconds, rounded up, so restarting twice does not drift backwards.
    Ok((latest.saturating_sub(wall).max(0) + 999) / 1_000 * 1_000)
}

pub fn has_data(engine: &Engine) -> bool {
    engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r)", [], |r| r.get(0)).unwrap_or(false)
}

pub fn offset_line(offset: i64, running: bool) -> String {
    format!("clock     offset from wall time: {offset:+}ms{}", if running { " (lasts until restart)" } else { " (no running dev; inspecting against wall time)" })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    fn engine() -> Engine {
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), "table alpha:\n  at: time\n  public\ntable beta:\n  at: time\n  public\n".into())]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema,
            bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p| p.program), 100_000).unwrap()
    }
    fn seed(engine: &mut Engine, table: &str, commit_at: i64, row_at: i64) {
        engine.seed_at([(table.into(), snapback4_core::value::object([
            ("id", snapback4_core::Value::Id(snapback4_core::ids::mint(row_at, &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default()).unwrap())),
            ("at", snapback4_core::Value::Int(row_at)),
        ]))], commit_at).unwrap();
    }
    #[test]
    fn clock_report_bounds_one_large_job_error_repeated_across_many_schedules() {
        let mut source = "table rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n".to_owned();
        for n in 0..500 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule s{n} every 1m: tick\n")); }
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program), 0).unwrap();
        engine.fire_schedules(0).unwrap();
        let before = saved_boundaries(&engine);
        assert_eq!(crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, 60_000, false, true).unwrap().len(), 500);
        let fired = engine.fire_schedules(60_000).unwrap();
        let message = "🧪\n\\".repeat(30_000);
        engine.store().conn().execute("UPDATE _jobs SET error = ?1 WHERE id = (SELECT id FROM _jobs ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1)", [&message]).unwrap();
        let report = schedule_report(&engine, &before, &fired, 60_000, false, false, None);
        assert_eq!(report.len(), 500);
        assert!(report.iter().all(|s| s["run"]["errorTruncated"] == true));
        assert!(report.iter().all(|s| s["run"]["error"].as_str().unwrap().len() <= REPORT_ERROR_BYTES + 3));
        assert!(serde_json::to_vec(&report).unwrap().len() < 2 << 20);
    }
    #[test]
    fn clock_report_refuses_before_the_host_encoder_limit() {
        let mut source = "table rows:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\njob tick():\n  for row in rows:\n    update rows[row.id] {n:1}\n".to_owned();
        for n in 0..3000 { source.push_str(&format!("schedule s{n} every 1m: tick\n")); }
        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
        let mut engine = Engine::new_at(snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap(), bound.schema, bound.programs.into_iter().map(|p|p.program), 0).unwrap();
        engine.fire_schedules(0).unwrap();
        let before = saved_boundaries(&engine);
        let refusal = crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, 60_000, false, true).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(refusal.code, "E_CLOCK_INPUT");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("byte budget"));
        assert_eq!(before, saved_boundaries(&engine));
        assert!(REPORT_BYTE_BUDGET < crate::serve::JSON_RESPONSE_CEILING);
    }
    #[test]
    fn utc_text_and_spans_match_the_calendar() {
        assert_eq!(utc(0), "1970-01-01T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(951_782_400_000), "2000-02-29T00:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(-1), "1969-12-31T23:59:59Z");
        assert_eq!(utc(1_791_468_000_000), "2026-10-08T14:00:00Z");
        assert_eq!(span(12_633_000), "3h30m33s");
        assert_eq!(span(86_400_001), "1d1s");
        assert_eq!(span(0), "0s");
    }
    #[test]
    fn resume_offset_follows_the_newest_commit_and_is_never_negative() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 100_000).unwrap(), 0);
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 3_700_500, 1);
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 100_000).unwrap(), 3_601_000);
        assert_eq!(resume_offset(engine.store(), 9_000_000).unwrap(), 0);
    }
    #[test]
    fn commit_tolerance_and_largest_lead_ignore_future_application_values() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 100_000, 1_000_000);
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().is_none());
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 105_000, 2_000_000);
        seed(&mut engine, "beta", 107_000, 3_000_000);
        let note = ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("up to 7000ms (latest 64 commits; tables: beta)"), "{note}");
        let note = ahead(&engine, 99_999).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("up to 7001ms (latest 64 commits; tables: alpha, beta)"), "{note}");
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 102_000).unwrap().is_none());
    }
    #[test]
    fn recent_window_does_not_claim_older_commits_or_read_row_payloads() {
        let mut engine = engine();
        seed(&mut engine, "alpha", 200_000, 200_000);
        for n in 0..RECENT_COMMITS { seed(&mut engine, "beta", 100_000, 300_000 + n as i64); }
        // Deliberately unreadable images: metadata inspection must not deserialize them.
        engine.store().conn().execute("UPDATE r SET data = 'not json'", []).unwrap();
        assert!(ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().is_none());
        engine.store().conn().execute("UPDATE _commits SET at = 200000, touched = ?1 WHERE seq = (SELECT MAX(seq) FROM _commits)", ["x".repeat(TABLE_BYTES + 1)]).unwrap();
        let note = ahead(&engine, 100_000).unwrap().unwrap();
        assert!(note.contains("100000ms") && note.contains("additional tables omitted"), "{note}");
    }
}

//! Business-state regressions for declarative day-two adoption.
use super::*;
use crate::{Context,Engine,WriteState,wire};
use crate::engine::release::ReleaseActivation;
use serde_json::{json,Value as Json};
fn compile(source:&str)->(Schema,Vec<crate::ir::Program>){
    let b=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.into())]);
    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
    (serde_json::from_str(&serde_json::to_string(&b.schema).unwrap()).unwrap(),b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|serde_json::from_str(&serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap()).unwrap()).collect())
}
fn engine(source:&str)->Engine{let(s,p)=compile(source);Engine::new_release(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,1,None).unwrap()}
fn adopt(e:&mut Engine,source:&str,n:u64)->Result<u64,RuntimeError>{
    let(s,p)=compile(source);let previous=e.store().meta("release").unwrap().map(|s|s.parse().unwrap());
    e.adopt_release(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(n,previous),false)
}
fn seed(e:&mut Engine,table:&str,row:Json){let row=wire::row_from_json(&e.schema().tables[table],&row).unwrap();e.seed([(table.into(),row)]).unwrap();}
fn row(e:&mut Engine,table:&str,id:&str)->Value{e.store_mut().transaction().unwrap().get(table,id).unwrap().unwrap()}
fn field(e:&mut Engine,table:&str,id:&str,c:&str)->Value{row(e,table,id).field(c).unwrap().clone()}
const BASE:&str="table bookings:\n  weight: int\n  length: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  insert <- allow\n  update <- allow\n  delete <- allow\n  online only\nmutation edit():\n  update bookings[id = 'a'] {weight:4000}\nmutation remove():\n  delete bookings[id = 'a']\n";
fn ferry()->String{BASE.replace("  weight: int","  vehicleClass: enum('car', 'van', 'commercial')\n  weight: int")+"backfill bookings vehicleClass(weight: int, length: int):\n  {vehicleClass: 'commercial' if weight > 3500 else 'van' if length >= 5 else 'car'}\nmutation classify():\n  update bookings[id = 'a'] {vehicleClass:'van'}\n"}
fn write(e:&mut Engine,op:&str){let r=e.mutate(op,None,Context::new("alice",Default::default())).unwrap();assert!(matches!(r.state,WriteState::Sent{..}),"{r:?}");}
#[test]
fn conditional_backfill_rollback_older_writes_and_forward_catchup(){
 let mut e=engine(BASE);seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","weight":1000,"length":4}));
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"b","weight":1000,"length":5}));
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"c","weight":4000,"length":4}));
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();
 for(id,class)in[("a","car"),("b","van"),("c","commercial")]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings",id,"vehicleClass"),Value::String(class.into()));}
 assert!(e.store().adoption_report.backfills.iter().any(|s|s.contains("transformed 3 rows")));
 let g=e.generation();adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
 write(&mut e,"classify");
 adopt(&mut e,BASE,1).unwrap();assert!(row(&mut e,"bookings","a").field("vehicleClass").is_err());
 seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"d","weight":3600,"length":1}));
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","vehicleClass"),Value::String("van".into()));
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","d","vehicleClass"),Value::String("commercial".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,BASE,1).unwrap();write(&mut e,"edit");
 let before=e.generation();let error=adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("edited")&&error.contains("historical inputs changed"),"{error}");assert_eq!(before,e.generation());
}
#[test]
fn older_input_change_recomputes_unedited_derived_value(){
 let mut e=engine(BASE);seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","weight":1000,"length":4}));
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();adopt(&mut e,BASE,1).unwrap();write(&mut e,"edit");
 adopt(&mut e,&ferry(),2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","vehicleClass"),Value::String("commercial".into()));
}
const CUE:&str="table cues:\n  status: enum('standby','done')\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\n  online only\n";
fn cues()->String{CUE.replace("enum('standby','done')","enum('standby','complete','skipped')")+"backfill cues rename(status: enum('standby','done')):\n  {status: 'complete' if status = 'done' else status}\nmutation skip():\n  update cues['a'] {status:'skipped'}\n"}
#[test]
fn enum_rename_reverses_exact_preimage_and_refuses_post_backfill_edits(){

 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("complete".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,CUE,1).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("done".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,&cues(),2).unwrap();write(&mut e,"skip");let g=e.generation();
 let error=adopt(&mut e,CUE,1).unwrap_err().to_string();assert!(error.contains("changed after backfill"),"{error}");assert_eq!(g,e.generation());
 assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"cues","a","status"),Value::String("skipped".into()));
}

fn string_split_and_ascii_digit_classification_are_deterministic(){
 let source="table people:\n  name: text <=100\n  location: text <=100\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=source.replace("  name:","  first: text <=100\n  last: text <=100\n  room: bool\n  name:")+"backfill people parts(name: text <=100, location: text <=100):\n  {first: beforeLast(name, ' '), last: afterLast(name, ' '), room: allDigits(location)}\n";
 let mut e=engine(source);for(id,name,location)in[("a","Ada Lovelace","123"),("b","Prince","１２３"),("c","A B C","")]{seed(&mut e,"people",json!({"id":id,"name":name,"location":location}));}
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();
 for(id,first,last,room)in[("a","Ada","Lovelace",true),("b","Prince","",false),("c","A B","C",false)]{assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"first"),Value::String(first.into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"last"),Value::String(last.into()));assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"people",id,"room"),Value::Bool(room));}
}

#[test]
fn all_fifty_wave17_change_shapes_run_without_migration_programs(){
 let cases:Vec<Json>=serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../tests/backfills/wave17.json")).unwrap();assert_eq!(cases.len(),50);
 for case in cases {
  let lane=case["lane"].as_u64().unwrap();let inputs=case["inputs"].as_object().unwrap();let outputs=case["outputs"].as_object().unwrap();
  let columns=|fields:&serde_json::Map<String,Json>|fields.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("  {c}: {}\n",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<String>();
  let old=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n",columns(inputs));
  let args=inputs.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("{c}: {}",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
  let next=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill rows change{lane}({args}):\n  {}\n",columns(outputs),case["expression"].as_str().unwrap());
  let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),next.clone())]);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(),"lane {lane} {}: {:?}\n{next}",case["slug"],bound.diagnostics);
  let mut e=engine(&old);
  for (i,r) in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){let mut input=r["input"].as_object().unwrap().clone();input.insert("id".into(),json!(format!("r{i}")));seed(&mut e,"rows",Json::Object(input));}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane}: {error}\n{next}"));
  assert!(e.schema().public_projection().backfills.is_empty(),"historical transforms never enter device input");
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){
   let got=row(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"));
   for(c,wanted)in r["expect"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(got.field(c).unwrap()),*wanted,"lane {lane} row {i} column {c}");}
  }
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} rollback: {error}"));
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){for(c,wanted)in r["input"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(&field(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"),c)),*wanted,"lane {lane} restored {c}");}}
  adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} forward: {error}"));
 }
}

fn unique_lookup_uses_historical_rows_and_rechecks_changed_lookup_values(){
 let old="table members:\n  principal: principal\n  plot: text <=24\n  unique principal\n  read <- deny\n  online only\ntable bookings:\n  owner: principal\n  public 'fixture'\n  online only\n";
 let next=old.replace("  owner: principal","  plot: text <=24\n  owner: principal")+"backfill bookings plot(owner: principal):\n  {plot:(members[principal = owner] ! else NOT_FOUND).plot}\n";
 let mut e=engine(old);seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"A17"}));seed(&mut e,"bookings",json!({"id":"a","owner":"user:a"}));
 adopt(&mut e,&next,2).unwrap();assert_eq!(field(&mut e,"bookings","a","plot"),Value::String("A17".into()));
 adopt(&mut e,old,1).unwrap();
 // Trusted seed replaces the source row as an operator update for this test.
 seed(&mut e,"members",json!({"id":"m","principal":"user:a","plot":"B20"}));

}

// Reset even when a debug/unwind request panics mid-drain. Engine poison
// recovery and durable work cursors retain the normal retry semantics.
struct Advancing<'a>(&'a Shared);
impl Drop for Advancing<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.0.maintenance);
        state.advancing = false;
        state.next = BulkKind::Delivery;
    }
}

fn completed_drain(work: Work, stopping: bool) -> Result<Work, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if stopping {
        return Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_CLOCK_INTERRUPTED", "clock", "clock advanced, but host shutdown interrupted the drain; inspect queued work instead of repeating the advance").guide("clock"));
    }
    Ok(work)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — staging grants clock control, never fixture or persona authority.
pub(super) fn staging(shared: &Shared) -> bool {
    lock(&shared.engine).store().meta("deployment_environment").ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging")
}
fn clock_service(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<Option<String>, snapback4_core::Refusal> {
    if shared.personas { return Ok(None); }
    if !staging(shared) { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
    match super::session_identity::principal_of(request,shared) {
        Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind == "service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Ok(Some(principal)),
        _ => Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "staging clock control requires a full service token").guide("clock")),
    }
}
pub(super) fn advance(request: Request, shared: &Shared, body: &str) {
    let service = match clock_service(&request, shared) {
        Ok(service) => service,
        Err(why) => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})),
    };
    let payload = match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(body) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))})),
    };
    let each_boundary = match payload.get("eachBoundary") {
        None => false,
        Some(serde_json::Value::Bool(value)) => *value,
        Some(_) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::input("eachBoundary must be a boolean"))})),
    };
    let duration = payload.get("duration").and_then(|value| value.as_str());
    let milliseconds = match duration.ok_or_else(|| crate::dev_clock::input("clock advance takes {\"duration\":\"+2m\"}"))
        .and_then(crate::dev_clock::duration) {
        Ok(milliseconds) => milliseconds,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // Serialize clock callers only. Background slices check advancing after
    // engine admission and skip during this synchronous drain.
    let advance = lock(&shared.maintenance).advance.clone();
    let _advance = lock(&advance);
    let _advancing = Advancing(shared);
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — simulated time makes retries due without stealing a live worker's claim or viewer session.
    let advanced = (|| {
        // Match claims' engine-then-clock lock order: no claim can land
        // between shifting its lease and publishing the advanced clock.
        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
        let before = crate::dev_clock_store::saved_boundaries(&engine);
        if let Some(principal) = &service {
            if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.refusal())?.as_deref() != Some("staging") { return Err(crate::dev_clock::release()); }
            super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request,shared,&engine,principal)?;
        } else { shared.dev_authority(&engine)?; }
        let mut clock = lock(&shared.clock);
        let mut next = clock.clone();
        let now = next.advance(milliseconds)?.max(shared.time_audience_clock.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire));
        let boundaries = crate::dev_clock_store::crossed_boundaries(&engine, &before, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary)?;
        lock(&shared.maintenance).advancing = true;
        engine.advance_worker_leases_at(milliseconds, clock.now()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
        *clock = next;
        let offset = clock.offset();
        let skew = now - snapback4_core::now_ms();
        let isolated = if crate::dev_clock_store::has_data(&engine) {
            format!("; use {} for isolated time tests; clock advance --port N targets its printed port (guide jobs)", crate::dev_clock::isolated_command())
        } else { String::new() };
        let message = format!("cumulative offset {offset:+}ms (requested process offset; now includes the authoritative floor); process offset lasts until restart{isolated}");
        Ok((now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries))
    })();
    let (now, offset, skew, message, before, boundaries) = match advanced {
        Ok(result) => result,
        Err(error) => return respond(request, 400, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    };
    // @ref LLP 3000#contended-read-serving — a developer is awaiting this
    // request; synchronous clock-advance work intentionally keeps request QoS.
    let drained = (|| {
        let mut crossed = Vec::new();
        if each_boundary {
            for (index, at) in boundaries {
                crossed.extend(engine_work(shared, |engine, _| engine.fire_schedule_boundary_at(shared.now(), index, at))?);
            }
        }
        let mut work = work_at(shared, true, 0, Some(now))?;
        work.schedules += crossed.len();
        work.fired.extend(crossed);
        Ok::<_, RuntimeError>(work)
    })();
    match drained.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|work| completed_drain(work, crate::lifecycle::stopping())) {
        Ok(work) => {
            let schedules = crate::dev_clock_store::schedule_report(&lock(&shared.engine), &before, &work.fired, now, shared.hold_schedules, each_boundary, shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| i.address.port()));
            respond(request, 200, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "work":work, "schedules":schedules}))
        }
        // Time remains advanced if maintenance fails; callers must not
        // retry the advance blindly. The normal tick retries pending work.
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"now":now, "advancedMs":milliseconds, "offsetMs":offset, "skewMs":skew, "eachBoundary":each_boundary, "message":message, "denied":refusal_json(&error)})),
    }
}

/// `POST /dev/jobs/<job>` with `{"args":{..}}`: queue one run of a job as the
/// system on the dev clock and drive chunks until it is done or parked. This is
/// what `snapback4 run <job>` forwards to while dev owns the store.
pub(super) fn run_job(request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, body: &str) {
    use snapback4_core::{JobState, error::Refusal, ir::ProgramKind, wire};
    let service_route=request.url().starts_with("/jobs/");
    let service=if service_route {
        match super::session_identity::principal_of(&request,shared) {
            Ok((principal,kind,snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full)) if kind=="service" && principal.starts_with("service:") => Some(principal),
            _ => return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","triggering a deployed system job requires a full service token").guide("jobs"))})),
        }
    } else {
        if !shared.personas {return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH","auth","/dev/jobs is a development door; use run <job> --release <dir> with a service token").guide("jobs"))}));}
        None
    };
    let refuse = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| respond(request, status, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal.guide("jobs"))}));
    let payload: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(if body.trim().is_empty() { "{}" } else { body }) {
        Ok(payload) => payload,
        Err(error) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body is not JSON: {error}; send {{\"args\":{{..}}}}"))),
    };
    if service_route && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
    let program = lock(&shared.bound).programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == name).map(|p| p.program.clone());
    let program = match program {
        Some(program) if program.kind == ProgramKind::Job => program,
        Some(other) => return refuse(request, 404, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{name} is a {}, not a job; queries are POST /q/<op>, mutations POST /m/<op>", other.kind.word()))),
        None => return respond(request, 404, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&super::dev_doors::stale(shared, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("unknown job {name}")).guide("jobs")))})),
    };
    if let Some(raw)=payload.get("args") && let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,raw) {return refuse(request,400,why);}
    let mut args = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    if let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(serde_json::Value::as_object) {
        for (field, value) in fields {
            let Some(kind) = program.args.get(field) else { return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{name} takes no argument {field}"))); };
            match wire::from_json(kind, value) {
                Ok(typed) => { args.insert(field.clone(), typed); }
                Err(why) => return refuse(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("argument {field}: {why}"))),
            }
//! Host startup; request scheduling remains in serve.rs.
use super::*;
pub fn serve(
    project: Project,
    port: u16,
    mut mode: Mode,
    bind: &str,
    inherited: Option<std::net::TcpListener>,
    capacity: crate::host_limits::Capacity,
) -> Result<(), String> {
    crate::host_limits::initialize();
    let capacity = capacity.fit()?;
    crate::host_limits::install(capacity);
    let mut source_stamp = project.sources_stamp();
    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        let verbose = !matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: false, .. });
        let (reports, hidden) = crate::verbs::problem_reports(&bound, true, verbose);
        let shown = reports.len();
        for report in reports {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{report}");
        }
        if hidden > 0 {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!(
                "snapback4 dev: {hidden} redundant cascade diagnostic(s) hidden; use --verbose to show them"
            );
        }
        return Err(format!(
            "{shown} problem{} in snapback/",
            if shown == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
        ));
    }
    let dev = matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { .. });
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — replay suppresses new effects and holds schedule admission; ordinary holds keep their separate meanings.
    let replay = matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { replay: true, .. });
    let hold_effects = matches!(
        mode,
        Mode::Dev {
            hold_effects: true,
            ..
        }
    );
    let hold_schedules = replay
        || matches!(
            mode,
            Mode::Dev {
                hold_schedules: true,
                ..
            }
        );
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.002#4-staging-clock — only immutable store classification admits a release test clock.
    let staging = if let Mode::Release { owner, .. } = &mode {
        owner.open_store(Duration::from_secs(5)).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.meta("deployment_environment").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.as_deref() == Some("staging")
    } else { false };
    let clock = crate::dev_clock::Clock::new(
        dev || staging,
        match mode {
            Mode::Dev { clock_offset, .. } | Mode::Release { clock_offset, .. } => clock_offset,
        },
    )
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let generated = if dev {
        Some(project.write_generated(&bound).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    if dev {
        project.check_server_at(&mut source_stamp)?;
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.2 — only the owning host may replace replay/schedule policy; deployment shares the same lock.
    // Release startup already owns this lock before selecting its source.
    let mut host_lock = if matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: false, .. }) {
        Some(crate::store_owner::host(&project.store_path())?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    // A busy port is a failed start, not permission to change the stopped host's policy.
    let server =
        crate::witness_socket::server(bind, port, inherited, capacity.connections(), || {
            crate::listen_refusal::in_use(bind, port, if dev { "dev" } else { "serve" })
        })?;
    let address = server
        .server_addr()
        .to_ip()
        .ok_or("HTTP listener has no IP address")?;
    if let Some(file) = &mut host_lock {
        crate::store_owner::announce(file, address)?;
    }
    if let Mode::Release { owner, .. } = &mut mode {
        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — publish through the acquired handle, including a release database symlink.
        crate::store_owner::announce(
            Arc::get_mut(owner).ok_or("release owner already shared")?,
            address,
        )?;
    }
    let host_owner = match &mode {
        Mode::Release { owner, .. } => Some(owner.clone()),
        _ => host_lock.take().map(Arc::new),
    };
    if let Some(owner) = &host_owner { owner.report_reclaim(matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { verbose: true, .. })); }
    // `--fresh` has private, disposable storage and the same WAL readers as dev.
    let fresh = if matches!(mode, Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. }) {
        Some(query_readers::FreshDirectory::new()?)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let mut engine = match &mode {
        Mode::Dev { fresh: true, .. } => Engine::new_at(
            fresh.as_ref().unwrap().store().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?,
            bound.schema.clone(),
            bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone()),
            clock.now(),
        ),
        Mode::Dev { .. } => adoption::open(host_owner.as_deref().unwrap(), &bound, &clock),
        Mode::Release { expected, .. } => host_owner
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap()
            .open_store(Duration::from_secs(5))
            .and_then(|store| project.open_release_engine(store, &bound, *expected, clock.now())),
    }
    .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    adoption::report(&engine);
    if dev {
        match crate::dev_clock_store::ahead(&engine, clock.now()) {
            Ok(Some(note)) => snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{note}"),
            Ok(None) => {}
            Err(error) => {
                snapback4_core::diagnostic!("clock     store-ahead inspection unavailable: {error}")
            }
        }
        for line in crate::dev_clock_store::held_schedules(&engine, clock.now()) {
            snapback4_core::diagnostic!("{line}");
        }
    }
    crate::dev_worker::clear_child_session(engine.store()).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    // A native job's module (LLP 3000.005): built by `check`, in `dev` also
    // here so a fresh clone serves without a separate step.
    if dev {
        crate::native::build(&project, &bound).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?;
    }
    if dev {
        crate::dev_replay::effects(&engine, replay).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        crate::dev_replay::schedules(&mut engine, hold_schedules, clock.now())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    if !hold_schedules {
        engine
            .fire_schedules(clock.now())
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    }
    let installed = crate::native::install(&mut engine, &project).map_err(|p| p.to_string())?;
    let authoritative_now = engine
        .advance_time_audiences_at(clock.now())
        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let time_audience_clock = if engine.schema().tables.values().any(|table| table.not_before.is_some()) {
        authoritative_now
    } else {
        i64::MIN
    };
    let clock = Arc::new(Mutex::new(clock));
    let presence = Arc::new(Live::new(clock.clone()));
    engine.set_presence(Some(presence.clone()));
    let seq = engine.seq().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let generation: u64 = engine.generation();
    let adoption = Mutex::new(adoption::Status::new(
        engine
            .store()
            .meta("store_id")
            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?
            .unwrap_or_default(),
    ));
    // Keep registration custody in this serving frame, independently of worker Arcs.
    let registration = if dev { Some(crate::dev_hosts::register(&project.root, address,
        engine.store().meta("store_id").map_err(|e|e.to_string())?.unwrap_or_default(),
        matches!(mode, Mode::Dev {fresh:true,..}))?) } else {None};
    let readers = Mutex::new(QueryReaders::new(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites));
    let shared = Arc::new(Shared {
        dev_reset_store: Mutex::new(None),
        dev_identity: registration.as_ref().map(|r| r.identity.clone()),
        release_requests: Arc::new(crate::release_requests::Gate::default()),

fn compile(source:&str)->(Schema,Vec<crate::ir::Program>){

    assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
    (serde_json::from_str(&serde_json::to_string(&b.schema).unwrap()).unwrap(),b.programs.into_iter().map(|p|serde_json::from_str(&serde_json::to_string(&p.program).unwrap()).unwrap()).collect())
}
fn engine(source:&str)->Engine{let(s,p)=compile(source);Engine::new_release(Store::memory().unwrap(),s,p,1,None).unwrap()}
fn adopt(e:&mut Engine,source:&str,n:u64)->Result<u64,RuntimeError>{
    let(s,p)=compile(source);let previous=e.store().meta("release").unwrap().map(|s|s.parse().unwrap());
    e.adopt_release(s,p,&ReleaseActivation::new(n,previous),false)
}

fn all_fifty_wave17_change_shapes_run_without_migration_programs(){
 let cases:Vec<Json>=serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../tests/backfills/wave17.json")).unwrap();assert_eq!(cases.len(),50);
 for case in cases {

  let columns=|fields:&serde_json::Map<String,Json>|fields.iter().map(|(c,t)|format!("  {c}: {}\n",t.as_str().unwrap())).collect::<String>();

  let next=format!("table rows:\n  legacy: bool default true\n{}  public 'fixture'\n  online only\nbackfill rows change{lane}({args}):\n  {}\n",columns(outputs),case["expression"].as_str().unwrap());

  }
  adopt(&mut e,&old,1).unwrap_or_else(|error|panic!("lane {lane} rollback: {error}"));
  for(i,r)in case["rows"].as_array().unwrap().iter().enumerate(){for(c,wanted)in r["input"].as_object().unwrap(){assert_eq!(crate::wire::to_json(&field(&mut e,"rows",&format!("r{i}"),c)),*wanted,"lane {lane} restored {c}");}}

//! Candidate activation: project rows, validate invariants, build indexes
//! and derived rows, then publish the semantic generation in one commit.
// @ref LLP 3000.000#implementation-follow-up — one atomic activation boundary

pub(super) use super::activation_walk::walk;
use super::activation_walk::walk_metered;
use super::*;
use crate::ir::Program;
use crate::schema::{Constraint, materialize_optional_fields};
use crate::value::digest;
use std::collections::BTreeSet;

pub(super) type Identity = (Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>, Option<String>,
);

pub(super) fn identity(conn: &Connection) -> Result<Identity, RuntimeError> {
    let mut statement = conn.prepare_cached("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1")?;
    let mut get = |key| statement.query_row([key], |row| row.get(0)).optional();
    Ok((get("schema_hash")?, get("activation_hash")?, get("generation")?, get("device:generation")?,
    ))
}

impl Store {
    /// Install a device's schema. Server engines also supply their programs
    /// and derived-state builder through `activate` below.
    pub fn install(&mut self, schema: &Schema) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.activate(schema, None, false, |_, _| Ok(())).map(|_| ())
    }

    /// A device can discard an obsolete partition while atomically caching
    /// its replacement backend and reset watermark. It never migrates rows
    /// whose authority belongs to a previous server generation.
    pub fn install_device(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked(schema, metadata, reset, &BTreeMap::new())
    }

    pub fn install_device_checked(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked_with(schema, metadata, reset, expected, |_| Ok(()))
    }

    /// Device-owned sidecar changes share the schema installation transaction.
    pub fn install_device_checked_with(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
        self.install_device_checked_with_finish(schema, metadata, reset, expected, prepare, |_| {
            Ok(())
        })
    }

    /// Opening recovery and its final reads must finish before installation commits.
    pub fn install_device_checked_with_finish<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, metadata: &BTreeMap<String, String>, reset: bool, expected: &BTreeMap<String, Option<String>>, prepare: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<R, RuntimeError> {
        let mut result = None;
        self.activate(schema, None, reset, |tx, _| {
            for (key, value) in expected {
                let held: Option<String> = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = ?1", [key], |row| {
                        row.get(0)
                    }).optional()?;
                if &held != value { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("the backend adoption capture is obsolete; retry sync".into())); }
            }
            prepare(tx)?;
            for (key, value) in metadata {
                // A device metadata namespace reset shares backend installation's transaction.
                if key.ends_with(':') {
                    tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _meta WHERE key >= ?1 AND key < ?2", params![key, format!("{key}\u{10ffff}")])?;
                } else { tx.set_meta(key, value)?; }
            }
            if reset { tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _device_base; DELETE FROM _device_snapshot;")?; }
            result = Some(finish(tx)?);
            Ok(())
        })?;
        Ok(result.expect("activation ran opening recovery"))
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.activate_at(schema, programs, reset, crate::now_ms(), |tx, changed, _| build(tx, changed))
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate_at(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        // One-shot device preparation is never retried or given a larger cache.
        self.activate_transaction(schema, programs, reset, now, true, build)
    }

    pub(crate) fn activate_at_with_launch<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl Fn(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, mut build: impl FnMut(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl Fn(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        let budget = if programs.is_some() { super::activation_cache::configured_mib()? } else { 0 };
        // The server builder owns only transaction-local work. Recreate all of
        // it on retry, with the same schema/programs/activation time.
        self.with_activation_cache(budget, |store| {
            store.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, &check, &mut build, &finish)
        })
    }

    pub(super) fn activate_transaction(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
        self.activate_transaction_with(schema, programs, reset, now, allow_launch, |_| Ok(()), build, |_| Ok(())).map(|(generation, ())| generation)
    }

    fn activate_transaction_with<R>(&mut self, schema: &Schema, programs: Option<&BTreeMap<String, Program>>, reset: bool, now: i64, allow_launch: bool, check: impl FnOnce(&Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, build: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>, bool, &mut super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport) -> Result<(), RuntimeError>, finish: impl FnOnce(&Tx<'_>) -> Result<R, RuntimeError>,
    ) -> Result<(u64, R), RuntimeError> {
        super::live_activation::configure(&self.conn,schema)?;
        let mut report = super::adoption_report::AdoptionReport::default();
        let mut phase = std::time::Instant::now();
        if programs.is_some() {
            if let Some(estimate) = self.adoption_estimate(schema)?.filter(|e| e.seconds().1 > 1.0) {
                crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic adoption: {}", estimate.summary());
            }
            let estimates = self.check_timeline_activation_at(schema, now, allow_launch)?;
            for estimate in estimates.iter().filter(|e| e.sources != 0) { crate::diagnostic!("snapback4: atomic feed adoption: {}", estimate.summary()); }
        }
        report.phase("read-only advice", &mut phase);
        self.check_ownership()?;
        let sql = self.conn.transaction_with_behavior(rusqlite::TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
        // Low-level preparation may precede a writer-lock wait. Recheck all
        // opening admission under the lock that owns the upgrades.
        Self::check_version(&sql)?;
        let before = identity(&sql)?;
        if before != self.identity { return Err(RuntimeError::Generation("another engine activated this store; reopen it before activating source".into())); }
        check(&sql)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.3 — physical upgrades roll back with adoption.
        // Rollback restores schema/rows and freelist count. It may leave
        // changed free-page contents or journal/WAL residue, not committed data.
        Self::initialize_schema(&sql)?;
        if programs.is_some() { Self::initialize_change_evidence(&sql)?; }
        index_key_guards(&sql)?;
        // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — reuse an admitted key bound, never assume a legacy store was checked.
        // Physical INSERT/UPDATE triggers invalidate the proof on oversized keys,
        // including direct SQL. Every activation key
        // below is checked too. Bind the proof to the predecessor so an older
        // binary's adoption invalidates it; device installation always scans.
        let key_proof = before.1.as_ref().map(|hash| format!("{}:{hash}", crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES));
        let validated: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'index_keys_validated'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let keys_validated = programs.is_some() && key_proof.is_some() && validated == key_proof;
        #[cfg(test)]
        let keys_validated = keys_validated && !self.full_adoption;
        // One bounded result, no row images. Fresh stores have no schema;
        // an explicit device reset discards the old entries in this transaction.
        if before.0.is_some() && !reset && !keys_validated {
            let violation: Option<(String, i64)> = sql.query_row(
                "SELECT c.name, COUNT(*) FROM i JOIN _catalog c ON c.num = i.ti WHERE length(i.k) > ?1 GROUP BY i.ti ORDER BY i.ti LIMIT 1",
                [crate::rows::MAX_INDEX_KEY_BYTES as i64], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?))
            ).optional()?;
            if let Some((name, count)) = violation {
                let (table, index) = name.rsplit_once('.').unwrap_or((&name, "unknown"));
                return Err(crate::rows::index_key_refusal(table, index, count as u64));
            }
        }
        report.phase("key validation", &mut phase);
        let schema_hash = schema.hash();
        // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#6-devices-and-partitions — executable native content changes the existing semantic generation.
        let native: Option<String> = sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key='release_native_digest'", [], |r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
        let activation_hash = programs.map(|programs| match &native {Some(native)=>digest(&(schema,programs,native)),None=>digest(&(schema,programs))});
        let schema_changed = before.0.as_deref() != Some(&schema_hash);
        let mut changed = schema_changed || activation_hash.as_ref().is_some_and(|hash| before.1.as_ref() != Some(hash));
        // The address function is part of the semantic generation even when
        // the authored schema and programs are byte-identical. Devices reset
        // with that generation; the server rebuilds old target rows below.
        let predecessor_has_aggregate = sql.query_row(
            "SELECT schema FROM _generations WHERE generation = (SELECT CAST(value AS INTEGER) FROM _meta WHERE key = 'generation')",
            [], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0),
        ).optional()?.map(|text| serde_json::from_str::<Schema>(&text)
            .map_err(|error| RuntimeError::decode(error, RuntimeError::Store))).transpose()?
            .is_some_and(|previous| previous.maintains.iter().any(|maintain| maintain.row_key.is_none()));
        let maintained_identity_upgrade = programs.is_some()
            && predecessor_has_aggregate
            && sql.query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key=?1", [crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_META], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0)).optional()?.as_deref()
                != Some(crate::schema::MAINTAINED_ID_VERSION);
        changed |= maintained_identity_upgrade;
        let mut catalog: BTreeMap<String, i64> = sql.prepare("SELECT name, num FROM _catalog")?
            .query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get(0)?, row.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_, _>>()?;
        // Repeat source-count admission under the writer lock, before any candidate writes.
        let preflight_catalog = catalog.clone();
        let (launches, sql) = {
            let preflight = Tx { activation_scan: None, changes: Default::default(), sequence_table: Default::default(), tx: sql, page_size: self.page_size,
                server: self.server,
                catalog: &preflight_catalog };

            let Tx { tx: sql, .. } = preflight;

        };
        let mut next = catalog.values().copied().max().unwrap_or(0) + 1;
        for (name, table) in schema.tables.iter().filter(|(_,t)| t.ephemeral.is_none()) {

        changed |= population_changed;

            }

                        && !old.maintained.contains_key(column) && !old.engine_field(column));

                    match default { crate::schema::ColumnDefault::Literal(value) => Some((column, value)), _ => None }
                }).collect();

                walk(&tx, catalog[name], |_, row| {
                    for (i,(column,_)) in fills.iter().enumerate() { if row.field(column).is_err() { counts[i] += 1; } }
