//! Release-only storage outside the active row/index namespace.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#2-retain-by-default — moves, restorations and deletion evidence share activation's transaction.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::{ColumnDefault, ColumnType, materialize_optional_fields};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};

pub(crate) const VERSION: &str = "snapback4-release-store-3";

#[derive(Serialize, Deserialize)]
struct Column {
    kind: ColumnType,
    default: Option<ColumnDefault>,
    authoritative: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    capture_source: Option<Table>,
}

pub(crate) fn enable(conn: &Connection) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    conn.execute_batch("CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_narrow_rows(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,reason TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_tables(table_name TEXT PRIMARY KEY, definition TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_rows(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(t,id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_columns(table_name TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(table_name,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_values(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,data TEXT NOT NULL,live_source INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_indexes(table_name TEXT NOT NULL,index_name TEXT NOT NULL,definition TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(table_name,index_name)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_refs(t INTEGER NOT NULL,id TEXT NOT NULL,column_name TEXT NOT NULL,parent_t INTEGER NOT NULL,parent_id TEXT NOT NULL,invalid INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,PRIMARY KEY(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id)) WITHOUT ROWID;
        CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS _dormant_parent ON _dormant_refs(parent_t,parent_id);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _release_events(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,at INTEGER NOT NULL,operation TEXT NOT NULL,release INTEGER,previous INTEGER,generation INTEGER NOT NULL,revision INTEGER NOT NULL,summary TEXT NOT NULL);
        CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS _release_event_items(event INTEGER NOT NULL,path TEXT NOT NULL,detail TEXT NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(event,path)) WITHOUT ROWID;")?;
    if !live_cells_column(conn)? {
        // Published predecessors have captured-only cells. Defaulting them
        // to captured is conservative; shared definitions cannot prove a
        // particular retained cell ever had live authority.
        conn.execute_batch("ALTER TABLE _dormant_values ADD COLUMN live_source INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0;")?;
    }
    conn.execute("UPDATE _meta SET value=?1 WHERE key='version' AND value!='snapback4-release-store-4'", [VERSION])?;
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn live_cells_column(conn:&Connection)->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM pragma_table_info('_dormant_values') WHERE name='live_source')",[],|r|r.get(0))?)
}

pub(super) fn enabled(tx: &Tx<'_>) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(matches!(tx.meta("version")?.as_deref(),Some(VERSION|"snapback4-release-store-2"|super::backfills::VERSION)))
}

fn authoritative(table: &Table, column: &str) -> bool {
    !table.maintained.contains_key(column) && !table.engine_field(column)
}

pub(super) fn definition(tx: &Tx<'_>, name: &str, table: &Table, column: &str) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let value = Column {
        kind: table.columns[column].clone(),
        default: table.defaults.get(column).cloned(),
        authoritative: authoritative(table, column),
        capture_source: table.engine_field(column).then(||table.clone()),
    };
    tx.conn().execute("INSERT INTO _dormant_columns VALUES(?1,?2,?3) ON CONFLICT(table_name,column_name) DO UPDATE SET definition=excluded.definition", params![name,column,canonical_json(&value)])?;
    Ok(())
}

fn references(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => references(inner, value, out),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) => {
            for value in values {
                references(of, value, out);
            }
        }
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn rewrite_legacy_reference(
    kind: &ColumnType,
    value: &mut Value,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> bool {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => {
            let Some(next) = ids.get(table).and_then(|ids| ids.get(id)) else { return false };
            *id = next.clone();
            true
        }
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), value) => rewrite_legacy_reference(inner, value, ids),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(values)) =>
            values.iter_mut().fold(false, |changed, value| rewrite_legacy_reference(of, value, ids) || changed),
        _ => false,
    }
}

fn rewrite_legacy_row(
    table: &Table,
    row: &mut Value,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> bool {
    let (Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields)) = row else { return false };
    table.columns.iter().fold(false, |changed, (column, kind)| {
        fields.get_mut(column).is_some_and(|value| rewrite_legacy_reference(kind, value, ids)) || changed
    })
}

/// Rewrite references retained outside the active namespace alongside their
/// active counterparts. References whose aggregate target was itself retired
/// have no map and retain the ordinary dormant-lifetime refusal on restore.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.003#3.0 — identity adoption covers every surviving typed reference, including dormant storage.
pub(crate) fn rewrite_legacy_maintained_references(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    ids: &BTreeMap<String, BTreeMap<String, String>>,
) -> Result<u64, RuntimeError> {
    if ids.values().all(BTreeMap::is_empty) || !enabled(tx)? { return Ok(0); }
    let mut rewritten = 0_u64;

    let cells = tx.conn().prepare(
        "SELECT v.t,v.id,v.column_name,v.data,c.definition FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog k ON k.num=v.t JOIN _dormant_columns c ON c.table_name=k.name AND c.column_name=v.column_name ORDER BY v.t,v.id,v.column_name"
    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?, row.get::<_,String>(4)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    for (t, id, column, data, definition) in cells {
        let definition: Column = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut value: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_reference(&definition.kind, &mut value, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_values SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3 AND column_name=?4", params![canonical_json(&value),t,id,column])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    let rows = tx.conn().prepare(
        "SELECT r.t,r.id,r.data,d.definition FROM _dormant_rows r JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=r.t JOIN _dormant_tables d ON d.table_name=c.name ORDER BY r.t,r.id"
    )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
        .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?;
    for (t, id, data, definition) in rows {
        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    let narrowed = if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
        tx.conn().prepare(
            "SELECT t,id,data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows ORDER BY t,id"
        )?.query_map([], |row| Ok((row.get::<_,i64>(0)?, row.get::<_,String>(1)?, row.get::<_,String>(2)?, row.get::<_,String>(3)?)))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>,_>>()?
    } else { Vec::new() };
    for (t, id, data, definition) in narrowed {
        let table: Table = serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        let mut row: Value = serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e|RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        if rewrite_legacy_row(&table, &mut row, ids) {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _dormant_narrow_rows SET data=?1 WHERE t=?2 AND id=?3", params![canonical_json(&row),t,id])?;
            rewritten = rewritten.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    for (target, target_ids) in ids {
        if target_ids.is_empty() { continue; }
        let parent_t = tx.num(target)?;
        for (old, next) in target_ids {
            tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs(t,id,column_name,parent_t,parent_id,invalid) SELECT t,id,column_name,parent_t,?1,invalid FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?2 AND parent_id=?3", params![next,parent_t,old])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE parent_t=?1 AND parent_id=?2", params![parent_t,old])?;
        }
    }
    Ok(rewritten)
}

pub(super) fn capture_refs(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    kind: &ColumnType,
    value: &Value,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    tx.conn().execute(
        "DELETE FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND column_name=?3",
        params![t, id, column],
    )?;
    let mut refs = Vec::new();
    references(kind, value, &mut refs);
    for (parent, parent_id) in refs {
        add_reference(tx, t, id, column, &parent, &parent_id)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn add_reference(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    t: i64,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    parent: &str,
    parent_id: &str,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    let parent_t = tx.num(parent)?;
    let invalid = !tx.conn().query_row("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM r WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",params![parent_t,parent_id],|r|r.get::<_,bool>(0))?
        && !tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2)",
            params![parent_t, parent_id],
            |r| r.get::<_, bool>(0),
        )?;
    tx.conn().execute(
        "INSERT OR IGNORE INTO _dormant_refs VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,?5,?6)",
        params![t, id, column, parent_t, parent_id, if invalid {2} else {0}],
    )?;
    Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn capture_through_parent(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    name: &str,
    table: &Table,
    id: &str,
    column: &str,
    row: &Value,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    if let Some(through) = &table.through {
        if (through.fields.contains_key(column)
            || matches!(
                column,
                crate::schema::through::PARENT_DEADLINE | crate::schema::through::DEADLINE
            ))
            && let Ok(Value::Id(parent_id)) = row.field(&through.reference)
        {
            add_reference(tx, tx.num(name)?, id, column, &through.parent, parent_id)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

  return host.then(host => ({ ...host, personas: host.personas!, now: async () => {
    const { response, body } = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/dev/status`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!response.ok || typeof body.now !== "number") throw Error("proof dev clock unavailable; password-only dev requires explicit --unsafe-dev-doors for clock/fixture tools; use serve staging for real users");
    return body.now;
  } }));
}
export interface ServeHost extends ProofHost {
  /** Authoritative staging time; requires a staging store and local operator authority. */
  now(): Promise<number>;
  /** Advance staging time once and drain due work. A lost reply must not be retried. */
  advance(duration: string, options?: { eachBoundary?: boolean }): Promise<number>;
  operatorToken(service?: string, days?: number): Promise<string>;
  seedAccounts(options: SeedAccountsOptions): Promise<StagingSessions>;
  sessions?: StagingSessions;
}
export interface ServeOptions extends ProofOptions { seedUsers?: SeedAccountsOptions; clockOffset?: string }
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#2-real-staging-accounts — operator authority stays in the existing local store proof; bulk setup refuses production.
/** @throws E_PROOF_USAGE for missing deployment directory; child exit, readiness deadline or served identity mismatch. */
export async function startServe(deployDir: string, options: ServeOptions | readonly string[] = {}): Promise<ServeHost> {
  if (typeof deployDir !== "string" || !deployDir.trim()) throw Object.assign(new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: startServe(deployDir, options) requires a deployment directory as its first argument"), { code: "E_PROOF_USAGE" });
  const opts: ServeOptions = Array.isArray(options) ? { args: options } : options as ServeOptions;
  const directory = resolve(opts.cwd ?? ".", deployDir);
  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && (typeof opts.clockOffset !== "string" || !/^[+-]?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(opts.clockOffset))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: clockOffset is a signed duration such as +25h");
  if (opts.clockOffset !== undefined && opts.args?.some(arg => arg === "--clock-offset" || arg.startsWith("--clock-offset="))) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: pass clockOffset once");
  const host = await nativeHost("serve", [directory], { ...opts, args: [...(opts.args ?? []), ...(opts.clockOffset === undefined ? [] : ["--clock-offset", opts.clockOffset])] });
  const clockCall = async (body?: object): Promise<number> => {
    const config = await fetchIdentity(`${host.url}/auth/config`, deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 10_000));
    if (!config.response.ok || config.body.environment !== "staging") throw Error("E_CLOCK_DEV_ONLY: clock controls require a deploy --staging store");
    const clockToken = served.operatorToken("clock");
    const response = await fetch(`${host.url}/dev/clock/${body ? "advance" : "status"}`, { method: body ? "POST" : "GET", headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${await clockToken}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" }, ...(body ? { body: JSON.stringify(body) } : {}), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(deadline(opts.timeoutMs, 60_000)) });
    const result = await response.json() as { now?: number; denied?: { code?: string; message?: string } };
    if (!response.ok || result.denied || !Number.isSafeInteger(result.now)) throw Error(`staging clock refused or reply unavailable; inspect clock and queued work before retrying: ${result.denied?.code ?? response.status} ${result.denied?.message ?? ""}`);
    return result.now!;
  };
  const served: ServeHost = { ...host,
    now: () => clockCall(),
    advance: (duration, options = {}) => {
      if (typeof duration !== "string" || !/^\+?\d+(ms|s|m|h|d|w)$/.test(duration)) throw new TypeError("E_PROOF_USAGE: advance requires a positive whole duration");
      return clockCall({ duration, eachBoundary: options.eachBoundary ?? false });
    },
    operatorToken: async (service = "operator", days = 1) => {
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}$/.test(service) || !Number.isSafeInteger(days) || days < 1 || days > 3650) throw Error("proof operatorToken: invalid service or days (1..3650)");
      const result = await cli(["token", "--service", service, "--days", String(days), "--release", directory, "--quiet"], opts);
      if (result.code !== 0) throw Error(`proof operator token refused: ${result.stderr}`);
      const token = result.stdout.trim();
      if (!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token)) throw Error("proof operator token malformed");
      return token;
    },
    seedAccounts: accountOptions => seedAccounts(served, accountOptions),
  };
  try { if (opts.seedUsers) served.sessions = await served.seedAccounts(opts.seedUsers); return served; }
  catch (error) { await served.stop(); throw error; }
}
async function frontend(kind: "static" | "vite", dir: string, options: ProofOptions): Promise<ProofHost> {

    const url = await ready(run, line => {

      if (!match) return;

      return data.url as string;

    if (response.headers.get("x-snapback4-proof") !== String(run.child.pid) + ":" + run.id) throw Error("proof frontend identity mismatch");
    await response.body?.cancel();

}

pub(super) fn clock_status(request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    if let Err(why) = clock_service(&request, shared) { return respond(request,403,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})); }
    let result = (|| {
        let (epoch, reader) = shared.reader()?;
        let note = crate::dev_clock_store::ahead(&reader, shared.now());
        lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
        note
    })();
    match result {
        Ok(note) => { let now = shared.now(); respond(request, 200, json!({"storeAhead":note,"now":now,"skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms()})) },
        Err(error) => respond(request, 500, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&error.refusal())})),
    }
}

        return routes::ingress(request, shared, &name, route);
    }
    let ephemeral_request = method == Method::Post && path.strip_prefix("/m/").is_some_and(|op| lock(&shared.bound).schema.operations.get(op).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral));
    let reject = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| {
        if ephemeral_request { ephemeral::failed(request,shared,None,refusal); }
        else if path.starts_with("/q/") { operation_refusal(request, shared, status, refusal); }
        else { respond_timed(request,shared,status,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
    };
    if let Err(refusal) = dev_request(&request, shared) {
        if ephemeral_request { return ephemeral::failed(request, shared, None, refusal); }
        respond_with_sync(request, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }), false, None, Some(shared));
        return;
    }
    // /dev/token has already been routed through its mutual local-owner proof.
    if shared.personas && (path.starts_with("/dev/") || path == "/seed") && !shared.dev_doors() {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&dev_doors::closed())}));
    }
    if path.starts_with("/dev/clock") && !shared.personas && !maintenance::staging(shared) {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release()) }));
    }
    // Discovered control requests bind to the inspected serving instance.
    // A listener replaced between GET status and POST cannot receive the action.
    if method==Method::Post && (matches!(path.as_str(), "/dev/clock/advance"|"/dev/reset"|"/dev/latency/reset") || (shared.personas && path=="/seed")) {
        match single_header(&request,"x-snapback-dev-instance") {
            Ok(None)=>{},
            Ok(Some(instance)) if shared.dev_identity.as_ref().is_some_and(|i| i.instance==instance)=>{},
            _=>return respond_timed(request,shared,409,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","dev instance changed since discovery; rerun the command to select the live host"))})),

    }
    if (method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/latency") || (method == Method::Post && path == "/dev/latency/reset") {
        return latency::endpoint(request, shared, method == Method::Post);
    }
    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#3-proof-fixture-loading — development field types need no principal.
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/catalog" {
        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "fixture catalog requires dev"))})); }
        let bound = lock(&shared.bound);

        let now = shared.now();
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"instance":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.instance), "initialStoreId":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.initial_store_id), "pid":std::process::id(), "wrapperPid":crate::supervisor::pid(), "storeId":status.store_id, "project":shared.project, "temporary":shared.temporary, "generation":generation, "adoption":status.failure, "now":now, "clockOffsetMs":lock(&shared.clock).offset(), "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "personas":personas, "scheduling":crate::thread_priority::diagnostics(), "fileDescriptors":crate::host_limits::diagnostics()}));
    }
    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/clock/status" { return maintenance::clock_status(request, shared); }
    if method == Method::Options {
        respond_empty(request, 204);
        return;
    }
    if method == Method::Post && path == "/seed" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
    }
    if path == "/dev/reset" && !shared.personas {
        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&dev_doors::reset_release()) }));
    }

//! Release row lifetimes whose present values cannot inhabit the selected type.
// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#91-per-row-narrowed-value-dormancy — hide a reference-closed population; never coerce values.
use super::*;
use crate::schema::{ColumnType, Constraint};

pub(super) fn present(conn: &Connection) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    Ok(conn.query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_schema WHERE name='_dormant_narrow_rows')",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?)
}

fn incompatible(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema, name: &str, row: &Value) -> Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    incompatible_except(tx,schema,name,row,&[])
}
fn incompatible_except(tx:&Tx<'_>,schema: &Schema,name:&str,row:&Value,authority:&[String])->Result<bool,RuntimeError> {
    let table = &schema.tables[name];
    for (column,kind) in &table.columns {
        if table.engine_field(column) || table.maintained.contains_key(column) || authority.contains(column) {continue;}
        if let Ok(value)=row.field(column) {
            if incompatible_column(tx, table, name, column, kind, value)? { return Ok(true); }
        }
    }
    Ok(false)
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#11-wave-11-factual-release-narration — the warning and activation share the value predicate, including ledger-backed assets.
pub(super) fn incompatible_column(tx: &Tx<'_>, table: &Table, name: &str, column: &str, kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value) -> Result<bool, RuntimeError> {
    if crate::engine::validate::validate_column(name, column, kind, Some(value)).is_err() { return Ok(true); }
    // @ref LLP 3000.038#1-the-type — asset facts belong to the ledger, including optional/list leaves.
    match super::activation::validate_assets(tx, kind, Some(value)) {
        Err(RuntimeError::Asset { code: "E_ASSET_TYPE" | "E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", .. }) => return Ok(true),
        Err(error) => return Err(error),
        Ok(()) => (),
    }
    Ok(table.constraints.iter().any(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { field, min, format }
        if field == column && matches!(value, Value::String(s)
            if crate::engine::validate::validate_text(field, s, *min, *format).is_err()))))
}

fn refs(kind: &ColumnType, value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<(String, String)>) {
    match (kind, value) {
        (ColumnType::Optional(inner), v) => refs(inner, v, out),
        (ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }, Value::Array(vs)) => {
            for v in vs {
                refs(of, v, out);
            }
        }
        (ColumnType::Ref { table }, Value::Id(id)) => out.push((table.clone(), id.clone())),
        _ => {}
    }
}

pub(super) fn transition(
    tx: &Tx<'_>,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    now: i64,
    restored_images: bool,
    source_definitions: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
    authority_sources: &BTreeMap<String, Table>,
) -> Result<(), RuntimeError> {
    retire_sources(tx,next,previous,now)?;
    let have_dormant: bool = tx.conn().query_row(
        "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows)",
        [],
        |r| r.get(0),
    )?;
    let type_change = previous.is_some_and(|old| {
        next.tables.iter().any(|(name, t)| {
            old.table(name).is_some_and(|p| {
                t.columns
                    .iter()
                    .any(|(c, k)| p.columns.get(c).is_some_and(|old| old != k))
                    || t.constraints
                        .iter()
                        .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
                        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                        != p.constraints
                            .iter()
                            .filter(|c| matches!(c, Constraint::Text { .. }))
                            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            })
        })
    });
    if !have_dormant && !type_change && !restored_images {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let mut newly_retained = 0u64;
    // Stream on the primary key. Do not retain a store-sized list of images.
    let mut cursor = (0i64, String::new());
    loop {
        let item: Option<(i64,String,i64,i64)> = tx.conn().query_row(
            "SELECT t,id,octet_length(data),octet_length(definition) FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE (t,id)>(?1,?2) ORDER BY t,id LIMIT 1",
            params![cursor.0,cursor.1],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?,r.get(3)?))).optional()?;
        let Some((t,id,data_bytes,definition_bytes))=item else {break};
        // Retain one admitted provenance/image unit through decode, overlays,
        // reconciliation and encoding. Never own its blobs before this bound.
        let mut admission=crate::meter::Meter::new(crate::meter::Ceilings::job(),crate::meter::Cut::Abort);
        if super::live_activation::active(tx)? {
            admission.heap((data_bytes as u64).saturating_add(definition_bytes as u64).saturating_mul(64).saturating_add(8192))?;
        }
        let (data,definition):(String,String)=tx.conn().query_row("SELECT data,definition FROM _dormant_narrow_rows WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2",params![t,id],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?;
        cursor = (t, id.clone());
        let name: String =
            tx.conn()
                .query_row("SELECT name FROM _catalog WHERE num=?1", [t], |r| r.get(0))?;
        let Some(table) = next
            .table(&name)
            .filter(|t| t.ephemeral.is_none() && next.maintain_for(&name).is_none())
        else {
            continue;
        };
        let old: Table =
            serde_json::from_str(&definition).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        super::dormant::check_owner(tx, &name, &old, next)?;
        let mut row: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(&data).map_err(|e| RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
        crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row,&old);
        let authority=crate::engine::through_activation::manual_authority_fields(next,&name,&old);
        if incompatible_except(tx,next,&name,&row,&authority)? {
            continue;
        }
        // Retained source deadlines precede projection, including removal of read-through itself.
        if crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::retires_image(table,&old,&row,now) {
            tx.delete(&name,&old,&row)?;
            continue;
        }
        // Actual deletion/reuse never recaptures a different parent lifetime.
        let invalid_columns = tx
            .conn()
            .prepare_cached(
                "SELECT column_name FROM _dormant_refs WHERE t=?1 AND id=?2 AND invalid=1",
            )?
            .query_map(params![t, id], |r| r.get::<_, String>(0))?
            .collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
        let mut invalid=false;
        for c in &invalid_columns {
            if table.columns.contains_key(c) && !super::authority_origin::obsolete_ref(tx,t,&id,c,table,Some(&old))? {invalid=true;}
        }
        if invalid {
            continue;
        }
        if super::authority_origin::live_row(tx,t,&id,&old,table)? {
            admission.heap(crate::engine::through_activation::restored_parent_scratch(next,source_definitions,&name,&old).saturating_add(crate::rows::disclosed::decode_reservation(table).saturating_mul(4)))?;
        }
        super::authority_origin::record(tx,t,&id,&old)?;
        super::authority_origin::validate(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now,true)?;
        crate::engine::through_activation::forward_image(tx,next,authority_sources,&name,&old,&row,now)?;
        if old.through.as_ref().is_some_and(|e|e.live.is_some()) {tx.conn().execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO temp._live_restored(kind,t,col,id) VALUES('live-authority',?1,'',?2)",params![t,id])?;}
        for column in old.columns.keys() {
            if !table.columns.contains_key(column) {
                super::dormant::definition(tx, &name, &old, column)?;
                if let Ok(value) = row.field(column) {
                    super::dormant::save_cell(tx, &name, &old, &id, column, value, false)?;
                }
            }
        }
        if let Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) = &mut row {
            fields.retain(|c, _| table.columns.contains_key(c));
        }
        let values=super::dormant::restored_values(tx,t,&id,table,&mut admission)?;
        super::authority_origin::overlay(tx,t,&id,Some(&old),table,&mut row,values)?;
        if old.through.as_ref().is_none_or(|e|e.live.is_some()) {
            for column in table.columns.keys() {super::dormant::mark_live_column(tx,t,&id,column)?;}
        }
        crate::engine::through_activation::manual_image(tx,next,&name,&old,&mut row,now)?;
        if incompatible(tx,next,&name,&row)? {continue;}
        crate::schema::materialize_optional_fields(&mut row, table);
        // Missing required new fields are a genuine backfill refusal, not dormancy.
        tx.conn().execute(
            "INSERT INTO r(t,id,data) VALUES(?1,?2,?3)",
            params![t, id, canonical_json(&row)],
        )?;
    }
    tx.clear_activation_scan();
    // References to a hidden row hide their dependents, including memberships.
    loop {
        let mut removed = 0u64;
            },
        });
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn retire_sources(tx:&Tx<'_>,next:&Schema,previous:Option<&Schema>,now:i64)->Result<(),RuntimeError>{
    // An expired predecessor must not be archived and later revived without its lifetime.
    if let Some(previous) = previous {
        let retirement = crate::engine::through_activation::Retirement::new(next, previous, now);
        for (name, old) in &previous.tables {
            if next.tables.contains_key(name) && retirement.affects(name) {
                super::activation::walk(tx, tx.num(name)?, |_, row| {
                    if retirement.retires(name, &row) {
                        tx.delete(name, old, &row)?;
                    }
                    Ok(())
                })?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

//! The program IR: what the Quarry binder emits and the interpreter runs, on
//! the server and on a device alike. Copied from Snapback 2 (LLP 2000.000
//! §5.1) in its serialized form, so the conformance corpus loads unchanged;
//! the held-search descriptor, a receipt-era form, is dropped.

pub mod effects;

use crate::value::Value;
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

fn is_zero(value: &u32) -> bool {
    *value == 0
}

fn is_false(value: &bool) -> bool {
    !*value
}

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Program {
    pub name: String,
    pub kind: ProgramKind,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub args: BTreeMap<String, ArgType>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub read_time_granularity_ms: Option<u64>,
    pub body: Vec<Stmt>,
    /// Binder-marked top-level returned shape loop paths and their source read sites.
    /// Empty for all other programs, preserving their serialized form.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "BTreeMap::is_empty")]
    pub returned_shapes: BTreeMap<String, String>,
    /// A job whose body is the app's own wasm module, run by the engine's
    /// native host one chunk per commit (LLP 3000.005). The body is empty.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub native: bool,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub enum ProgramKind {
    Query,
    Mutation,
    #[serde(rename = "job")]
    Job,
    #[serde(rename = "action")]
    Action,
}

impl ProgramKind {
    pub fn word(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Query => "query",
            Self::Mutation => "mutation",
            Self::Job => "job",
            Self::Action => "action",
        }
pub type VariantId = [u32; 4];

/// A bounded range over an index prefix.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct Scan {
    pub site: String,
    pub table: String,
    pub index: String,
    pub prefix: Vec<IndexKeyExpr>,
    pub order: Order,
    pub take: Bound,
    /// A program existence test consumes at most its first match below page ownership.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub existence: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub after: Option<Box<Expr>>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub before: Option<Box<Expr>>,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub range: Option<Range>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub filter: Option<Box<Expr>>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_false")]
    pub opaque_cursor: bool,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub merge_site: Option<String>,
    pub provenance: KeyProvenance,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub variant: Option<crate::ir::VariantId>,
    /// Leading equalities moved into this prefix; zero keeps strict cursor scope.
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "is_zero")]
    pub cursor_pins: u32,
}

impl Scan {
    /// True only for an ordinary index ordered by this column after its pins.
    /// Kept shared by execution and the cost certificate.
    pub fn orders_field(&self, schema: &crate::schema::Schema, field: &str) -> bool {
        let Some(index) = schema.table(&self.table).and_then(|table| table.index(&self.index)) else { return false; };
        // Expiring maintained totals are refreshed while reading. Their
        // stored index value does not order the live value returned today.
        if field == "value" && schema.maintain_for(&self.table).is_some_and(|maintain|
            schema.table(&maintain.source).is_some_and(|source| source.expire.is_some())) { return false; }
        self.prefix.iter().all(|key| matches!(key, IndexKeyExpr::Scalar(_)))
            && matches!(index.components.get(self.prefix.len()), Some(crate::schema::IndexComponent::Column(column)) if column == field)
    }
}

/// A union owns its projected cursor descriptor, including when no branch survives.

pub struct ViewPosition {
    pub take: u32,
    pub order: Order,
    pub after: Option<Box<Expr>>,
    pub suffix: Vec<crate::cursor::CursorColumn>,
}

    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub view: Option<String>,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Vec::is_empty")]
    pub order_keys: Vec<String>,

use super::*;

/// Who is asking: a bearer session, or (in `dev`, from loopback) a persona.
pub(crate) fn principal_of(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
    principal_with_owner(request, shared, None)
}

fn principal_with_owner(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, owner: Option<&Engine>) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
    // Declared public bearer routes keep their existing development admission.
    if !(request.method() == &Method::Post && routes::declared(shared, request.url()).is_some()
        && !request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona"))) {
        dev_request(request, shared)?;
    }
    let mut persona = None;
    let mut bearer = None;
    for header in request.headers() {
        let name = header.field.as_str().as_str().to_ascii_lowercase();
        let value = header.value.as_str().trim();
        if name == "x-snapback-persona" {
            if persona.is_some()
                || value.is_empty()
                || !value.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '-')
            {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one valid x-snapback-persona header").guide("personas"));
            }
            persona = Some(value.to_owned());
        }
        if name == "authorization" {
            let Some(token) = crate::auth::parse_bearer(value) else {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send Authorization: Bearer <token>").guide("personas"));
            };
            if bearer.replace(token.to_owned()).is_some() {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one Authorization header").guide("personas"));
            }
        }
    }
    if persona.is_some() && bearer.is_some() {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send one credential: a development persona or a bearer token").guide("personas"));
    }
    if let Some(persona) = persona {
        if let Some(engine) = owner { shared.dev_authority(engine)?; }
        if shared.dev_doors() {
            // `use personas` is the cast: a test expecting an outsider to be
            // refused must not see it signed in. No declaration admits none.
            // (Taken briefly; the engine
            // guard, when held, is always acquired before this one.)
            let declared = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
            if !declared.contains(&persona) {
                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", format!(
                    "persona {persona} is not declared; `use personas {}` is this project's cast. Add {persona} to it (dev re-adopts on save) or send a declared persona", declared.join(", ")))
                    .rewrite(format!("use personas {}{persona}", if declared.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("{}, ", declared.join(", ")) })).guide("personas"));
            }
            return Ok((persona_principal(&persona), "persona".into(), snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full));
        }
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "personas are a development door; a deployed server takes bearer tokens (snapback4/auth)").guide("personas"));
    }
    if let Some(token) = bearer {
        // Reads may authenticate against the committed generation during adoption.
        // Keep write authentication behind the writer: time queued for adoption
        // must not preserve a session that expired or was revoked in that wait.
        let path = request.url().split('?').next().unwrap_or("");
        let read = detached_request(request.method(), path);
        let resolve = |engine: &Engine, identity: Option<&str>, detached: bool| {
            let now = shared.now();
            let sample = snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now, supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire) };
            let result = if detached {
                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_read_only_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample, identity)
            } else {
                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample)
            };
            let result=result.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|value|value.ok_or_else(||
                snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(engine.store(), &token, now, trusted_dev_request(request, shared))));
            if result.is_err() {crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());}
            result
        };
        return if let Some(engine) = owner { resolve(engine, None, false) }
        else if read { match shared.reader() {
            Ok((epoch, reader)) => {
                let identity = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
                let result = resolve(&reader, identity.as_deref(), true);
                lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
                result
            }
            Err(_) if shared.memory_store() => resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false),
            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
        } } else { resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false) };
    }
    Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "sign in: send a bearer token (snapback4/auth), or x-snapback-persona in development").guide("personas"))
}

// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#4-development-doors — a persona cannot cross from authentication into a replacement password-only reader.
pub(crate) fn persona_read_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    let Some(persona) = request.headers().iter().find(|header| header.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona")) else { return Ok(()) };
    shared.dev_authority(engine)?;
    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
    if (!shared.unsafe_dev_doors && bound.schema.auth.password && !bound.schema.auth.guests)
        || !bound.personas.iter().any(|name| name == persona.value.as_str().trim()) {
        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "persona authority changed while this read waited; sign in with a password or use the current declared development cast").guide("staging"));
    }
    // The cast is checked at this read's admission. Never hold its publication
    // mutex during I/O: concurrent ordinary writes still need to authenticate.
    // The engine transaction fences a replaced execution schema.
    Ok(())
}

// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — adoption's writer queue cannot retain expired bearer authority.
pub(crate) fn mutation_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine, principal: &str) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    if principal_with_owner(request, shared, Some(engine))?.0 == principal { Ok(()) }
    else { Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the request's session changed; sign in again").guide("personas")) }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — additions require an explicit decision here.
pub(super) fn read_permitted(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
    match method {
        Method::Get => matches!(path, "/changes" | "/probe" | "/schema") || path.starts_with("/assets/"),
        Method::Post => matches!(path, "/sync" | "/probe" | "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout") || path.starts_with("/q/"),
        _ => false,
    }
}
        }
        let workers:Vec<(String,String)>=tx.conn().prepare("SELECT key,value FROM _meta WHERE key='worker:status' OR key LIKE 'worker:status:%' ORDER BY key LIMIT 16")?.query_map([],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?)))?.collect::<Result<_,_>>()?;
        let previous_active: Option<u64> = if present(tx.conn())? {tx.conn().query_row("SELECT previous FROM _release_events WHERE release IS NOT NULL AND previous != release AND operation != 'prune' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",[],|r|r.get::<_,Option<i64>>(0)).optional()?.flatten().map(|n|n as u64)} else {None};
        let result = serde_json::json!({"previous_active":previous_active,"release":tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()),"revision":tx.meta("release_revision")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),"generation":tx.meta("generation")?.and_then(|v|v.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(1),"native_digest":tx.meta("release_native_digest")?,"events_after":events.last().map(|e|e["id"].clone()),"events":events,"items_after":inventory_page.last().map(|i|i["key"].clone()),"items":inventory_page,"event":event_id,"workers":workers});
        tx.rollback()?;
        Ok(result)
    }
    pub fn prune_dormant(
        &mut self,
        confirm: bool,
        expected_revision: Option<u64>,
    ) -> Result<serde_json::Value, RuntimeError> {
        let tx = self.transaction()?;
        let held = tx
            .meta("release_revision")?
            .and_then(|v| v.parse::<u64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);
        if expected_revision.is_some_and(|n| n != held) {
            return Err(RuntimeError::Generation(
                "release revision changed; run releases and retry prune".into(),
            ));
        }
        let items = inventory(tx.conn(), None, usize::MAX)?;
        if !confirm {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(
                serde_json::json!({"pruned":false,"items":items,"rewrite":"snapback4 prune <dir> --allow-destructive"}),
            );
        }
        if !present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.rollback()?;
            return Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":[],"revision":held}));
        }
        // Release only dormant homes; active application data is untouched.
        tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE home_table IN (SELECT table_name FROM _dormant_tables) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_values v JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=v.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND v.column_name=_assets.home_column AND v.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
        if super::narrowed::present(tx.conn())? {
            tx.conn().execute("UPDATE _assets SET released_at=COALESCE(released_at,?1) WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n JOIN _catalog c ON c.num=n.t WHERE c.name=_assets.home_table AND n.id=_assets.home_id)",[crate::now_ms()])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_values WHERE EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM _dormant_narrow_rows n WHERE n.t=_dormant_values.t AND n.id=_dormant_values.id)",[])?;
            tx.conn().execute("DELETE FROM _dormant_narrow_rows",[])?;
        }
        let revision = event(
            &tx,
            "prune",
            tx.meta("release")?.and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()),
            None,
            serde_json::json!({"dropped_items":items.len(),"bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.bytes).sum::<u64>(),"asset_bytes":items.iter().map(|i|i.asset_bytes).sum::<u64>()}),
            &items,
        )?;
        let event_id = tx.conn().last_insert_rowid();
        tx.conn().execute_batch("DELETE FROM _dormant_values;DELETE FROM _dormant_rows;DELETE FROM _dormant_refs;DELETE FROM _dormant_columns;DELETE FROM _dormant_indexes;DELETE FROM _dormant_tables;")?;
        tx.commit()?;
        Ok(serde_json::json!({"pruned":true,"items":items,"revision":revision,"event":event_id}))
    }
}

//! Column admission, maintained fields and authority/identity/delivery dependencies.
// @ref LLP 3000.007.001#2 — the binder and raw-schema admission share this walk.
use super::*;

pub const GROUP: &str = "$parent";

#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ColumnOwner {
    pub table: String,
    pub column: String,
}

impl ColumnOwner {
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { format!("{}.{}", self.table, self.column) }
    // Bounded labels also keep a first contribution independent of parent state.
    pub fn target(&self) -> String { format!("$column:{}", digest(&(&self.table, &self.column))) }
}

impl Schema {
    pub(crate) fn private_owner(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&ColumnOwner> {
        // Privacy also holds before admission: a duplicate ordinary declaration
        // must never shadow a private one in a raw schema.
        self.maintains.iter().filter(|m| m.target == name).find_map(|m| m.private.as_ref())
    }

    pub fn visible(&self, name: &str) -> bool { self.private_owner(name).is_none() && !self.is_recount_work(name) }

    pub fn visible_tables(&self) -> impl Iterator<Item = (&String, &Table)> {
        self.tables.iter().filter(|(name, _)| self.visible(name))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007.001#3.2 — identity remains on Table, which image-retention compares.
    pub fn public_projection(&self) -> Self {
        let mut schema = self.clone();
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        schema.backfills.clear();
        // Inspection provenance is catalog side metadata, never device input.
        #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
        { schema.authored_indexes = Default::default(); }
        schema.prepare_receipt_dependencies();
        schema.receipt_dependencies.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.source_reservations.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.tables.retain(|name, _| self.visible(name));
        schema.maintains.retain(|m| self.visible(&m.target));
        schema
    }

    pub fn refuse_private(&self, name: &str) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        if self.is_recount_work(name) { return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural {code:"E_VIEW_KEY",message:"recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()}); }
        if let Some(owner) = self.private_owner(name) {
            return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Structural { code: "E_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_PRIVATE",
                message: format!("{} is maintained by the engine; read the parent row or write the source rows (guide maintains)", owner.label()) });
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    /// A projected device schema deliberately has metadata without private maintains.
    /// Authoritative engines must have exactly one derivation for every owned field.
    pub fn validate_column_owners(&self) -> Result<(), crate::error::RuntimeError> {
        for (name,table) in self.visible_tables() {
            for column in table.maintained.keys() {
                if self.maintains.iter().filter(|m|m.private.as_ref().is_some_and(|o|&o.table == name && &o.column == column)).count() != 1 {
                    return Err(crate::error::RuntimeError::Schema(format!("{name}.{column} needs exactly one engine derivation; declare the column aggregate (guide maintains)")));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }
}

impl Maintain {
    pub fn group_fields(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        if self.private.is_some() { vec![GROUP.into()] } else { self.by.clone() }
    }

    pub fn derivation_identity(&self) -> String { digest(&(&self.kind, &self.source, &self.filter, &self.by)) }
    pub fn label(&self) -> String { self.private.as_ref().map_or_else(|| self.target.clone(), ColumnOwner::label) }
}

struct Walk<'a> { schema: &'a Schema, issues: Vec<SchemaIssue> }
impl Walk<'_> {
    fn issue(&mut self, path: &str, label: &str) {
        self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message: format!("maintained column {label} is depended on by {path}; use an ordinary column for authority, identity, lifetime or delivery, or a separate maintain for an aggregate (guide maintains)") });
    }
    fn target(&mut self, table: &str, path: &str) {
        if self.schema.is_recount_work(table) {self.issues.push(SchemaIssue {path:path.into(),message:"E_VIEW_KEY: recount state is engine-owned; read the row-keyed view instead (guide maintains)".into()});}
        if let Some(owner) = self.schema.private_owner(table) { self.issue(path, &owner.label()); }
    }
    fn field(&mut self, table: &str, field: &str, path: &str) {
        if let Some(message) = self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, field) {
            self.issues.push(SchemaIssue { path: path.into(), message });
        }
        if self.schema.table(table).is_some_and(|t| t.maintained.contains_key(field)) { self.issue(path, &format!("{table}.{field}")); }
    }
    fn term(&mut self, table: &str, term: &RuleTerm, path: &str) {
        if let RuleTerm::Field(field) | RuleTerm::OldField(field) | RuleTerm::NextField(field) = term { self.field(table, field, path); }
    }
    fn index(&mut self, table: &str, index: &str, path: &str) {
        self.target(table, path);
        if let Some(definition) = self.schema.table(table) && let Some(index) = definition.index(index) {

        Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) => fields.values().any(contains_system_principal),
        _ => false,
    }
}

/// Missing optional columns read as null; missing required columns with a
/// literal default read that value. Contextual defaults belong to inserts.
pub fn materialize_optional_fields(row: &mut Value, table: &Table) {
    let fields = match row {
        Value::Row(fields) | Value::Map(fields) => fields,
        _ => return,
    };
    for (name, kind) in &table.columns {
        if !fields.contains_key(name) {
            if table.maintained.contains_key(name) {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), Value::Int(0));
            } else if kind.is_optional() {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), Value::Null);
            } else if let Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value)) = table.defaults.get(name) {
                fields.insert(name.clone(), value.clone());
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Borrow complete images; materialize old images only when needed.
pub fn row_image<'a>(row: &'a Value, table: &Table) -> std::borrow::Cow<'a, Value> {
    if table.columns.keys().all(|column| row.field(column).is_ok()) { return std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(row); }
    let mut image = row.clone();
    materialize_optional_fields(&mut image, table);
    std::borrow::Cow::Owned(image)
}

/// The retirement phase shared by activation and its read-only occupancy
/// preview. Selection and the predecessor deadline predicate live here once;
/// checking a candidate must not count a row this phase would remove.
pub(crate) struct Retirement<'a> {
    affected: Vec<(String, &'a Table)>,
    now: i64,
}

impl<'a> Retirement<'a> {
    pub(crate) fn new(next: &Schema, previous: &'a Schema, now: i64) -> Self {
        let affected = next
            .through_order()
            .into_iter()
            .filter_map(|name| {
                let next = &next.tables[&name];
                let old = previous.table(&name)?;
                Self::changes_lifetime(next,old).then_some((name, old))
            })
            .collect();
        Self { affected, now }
    }

    fn changes_lifetime(next:&Table,old:&Table) -> bool {
        next.through != old.through || next.through.is_some() && (next.expire != old.expire || next.expire.as_ref().is_some_and(|field|old.defaults.get(field)!=next.defaults.get(field)))
    }

    pub(crate) fn retires_image(next:&Table,old:&Table,row:&Value,now:i64) -> bool {
        (next.through!=old.through || next.expire!=old.expire || old.expire.as_ref().is_some_and(|field|old.defaults.get(field)!=next.defaults.get(field))) && old.expired(row,now)
    }

    pub(crate) fn affects(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
        self.affected.iter().any(|(table, _)| table == name)
    }

    pub(crate) fn retires(&self, name: &str, row: &Value) -> bool {
        self.affected
            .iter()
            .find(|(table, _)| table == name)
            .is_some_and(|(_, old)| old.expired(row, self.now))
    }
}

/// Capture barrier for an authoritative adapter's uncommitted generation.
pub fn activate(
    tx: &dyn Rows,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    now: i64,
) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, u64>, RuntimeError> {
    activate_retained(tx, next, previous, now, &Default::default())
}

pub(crate) fn activate_retained(
    tx: &dyn Rows,
    next: &Schema,
    previous: Option<&Schema>,
    now: i64,
    retained: &std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
) -> Result<std::collections::BTreeMap<String, u64>, RuntimeError> {
    let mut shortened = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
    let Some(previous) = previous else {
        return Ok(shortened);
    };
    // Previous indexes and row images still exist. Every affected expired row
    // retires under its old deadline, before a new default or capture can revive it.
    let retirement = Retirement::new(next, previous, now);
    for (name, old) in retirement.affected.iter().rev() {
        let bounded=live_work(tx,name,Some(old),next)?;
        rows(tx, name, old, 0, bounded, None, |row| {
            if retirement.retires(name, &row) {
                tx.delete(name, old, &row)?;
            }
            Ok(())
        })?;
    }
    forward_sources(tx,next,&previous.tables,now)?;
    reconcile_manual(tx,next,&previous.tables,now)?;
    // Compare all predecessor images before any ancestor is recaptured.
    for (name, _) in &retirement.affected {
        let Some(t) = &next.tables[name].through else {
            continue;
        let kind = match decl.kind {
            ProgramKind::Query => ir::ProgramKind::Query,
            ProgramKind::Mutation => ir::ProgramKind::Mutation,
            ProgramKind::Job => ir::ProgramKind::Job,
        };
        let ownership = body.ownership;
        let returned_shapes = returned_shapes::marks(&body.code, &full_name, &shape_candidates);
        let program = Program { name: full_name, kind, args, read_time_granularity_ms: None, body: body.code, native: false, returned_shapes };
        // @ref LLP 3000.036#17.8 — a total behind its grant's guard is the holder's whole total, as the server computes it.
        let mut refused = false;
        for (site, diagnostic) in std::mem::take(&mut binder.pending_totals) {
            let guarded = ir::scans(&program).into_iter().filter(|scan| scan.site == site).all(|scan| snapback4_core::engine::rules::grant_dominated(schema, &program, scan));
            if !guarded { diagnostics.push(diagnostic); refused = true; }
        }
        if refused { return None; }
        let presence = if kind == ir::ProgramKind::Mutation {
            Some(super::residency::presence_facts(schema, &program, &ownership, &binder.presence_sources))
        } else { None };
        Some(BoundProgram { maintenance_scopes: binder.maintenance_scopes, statement_ends, page_sources: binder.page_sources, canonical_sources: binder.canonical_sources, constraint_domains: binder.constraint_domains, filters: binder.filters, view_types: binder.view_types, selections: binder.selections, sources: binder.sources, receipt_sources: binder.receipt_sources, receipt_returns: binder.receipt_returns, implicit_write_results: binder.implicit_write_results, timeline_scans: binder.timeline_scans, program, presence, module: module.to_owned(), args: arg_types, result, notes: binder.notes, site: decl.site.clone(), job_bound_helpers: binder.job_bound_helpers })
    }

    fn site(&mut self) -> String {
        self.sites += 1;
        format!("programs.{}.{}", self.name, self.sites - 1)
    }

    fn temp(&mut self, hint: &str) -> String {
        self.temps += 1;
        format!("${hint}{}", self.temps)
    }

    fn lookup(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Ty> {
        self.binding(name).map(|binding| &binding.ty)
    }

    fn binding(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&Binding> {
//! Seeks and row-local scan predicates.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.000.001#4 — retain the binder's actual choice for why, never infer a new lookup or rewrite.
    pub(super) fn record_selection(&mut self, site: &str, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, result: &str, reason: &str) {
        let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
        let bindings = index.columns.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(position, column)| {
            let key = if named { read.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column)) } else { read.keys.get(position) }?;
            Some(format!("{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value)))
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        let origin = if self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())) { "synthesized; " } else { "" };
        let source = if origin.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("; read: {}", self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(read))) };
        let mut selection = format!("selected {} ({bindings}); result: {result}; {origin}{reason}{source}", index.name);
        if !self.variants.is_empty() {
            let when = self.variants.iter().map(|(name, present)| format!("{} {}", name.trim_start_matches("$intentPresent:").trim_start_matches("$intentSearch:"), if *present { "present" } else { "absent" })).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
            selection = format!("when {when}: {selection}");
        }
        self.selections.entry(site.into()).and_modify(|prior| { if !prior.contains(&selection) { prior.push_str("; "); prior.push_str(&selection); } }).or_insert(selection);
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — a covered total unique key reads one row; additional named equalities check that row.
    pub(super) fn read_value(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Expr> {
        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
        for key in &read.keys { if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::super::row_views::predicate_field(info, field, &read.site)?; } }
        if !read.seeks.is_empty() { return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek needs a bounded page", "t[prefix, col >= start] first 50 by index")); }
        let mut point = read.clone();
        if point.keys.is_empty() && info.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) {
            point.keys.push(ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), site: read.site.clone() }, site: read.site.clone() });
            point.index = Some("byGroup".into());
        } else if point.keys.is_empty() {
            return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{}[] reads nothing; a point read needs a key", read.table), format!("{}[id]", read.table)));
        }
        let implicit_group = mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && point.index.is_none();
        if implicit_group { point.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
        let (mut keys, index, mut selection) = self.select_index(info, &point, mode, out)?;
        super::super::point_key::check(info, &index, read, &keys.iter().map(|(column, _, ty)|
            (column.as_deref(), !ty.is_optional() && *ty != Ty::Unknown)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), false)?;
        if implicit_group { selection = "complete maintained group key".into(); }
        let extra = keys.len() > index.columns.len();
        let mut equalities = Vec::new();
        if extra {
            for (column, value, ty) in &mut keys {
                let column = column.as_ref().expect("named key");
                if column != "id" && !info.columns.contains_key(column) {
                    return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
                }
                let expected = self.key_ty(info, &index, column);
                if !ty.comparable(&expected) { return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
                *value = self.snapshot(value.clone(), out);
                if !index.columns.contains(column) { equalities.push((column.clone(), value.clone())); }
            }
            keys.retain(|(column, _, _)| index.columns.contains(column.as_ref().unwrap()));
        }
        let key = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
        let location = self.site();
        self.record_selection(&location, read, &index, if mode == ReadMode::Exists { "exists" } else if info.maintained.is_some() { "scalar" } else { "row or null" }, &selection);
        self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (read.site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
        let mut point = Read { site: location, table: read.table.clone(), index: index.name, key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
        // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.4 — only a caller-proved existence read may use the whole owner partition.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && equalities.is_empty() {
            super::super::own_keys::existence(self.schema, &mut point);
        }
        if equalities.is_empty() { return Ok(if mode == ReadMode::Exists { Expr::Exists(point) } else { Expr::Get(point) }); }
        let row = self.temp("probe");
        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Get(point) });
        let mut condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied aggregate carries a null value, not a present group.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && info.maintained.is_some() {
            let present = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value")), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(present) };
        }
        for (column, value) in equalities {
            let test = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), &column)), right: Box::new(value) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(test) };
        }
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists { return Ok(condition); }
        out.push(Stmt::If { condition, then_body: Vec::new(), else_body: vec![Stmt::Assign { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) }] });
        Ok(ir::var(&row))
    }

snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/bind/program/reads.rs:119:    pub(super) fn variant(&self) -> Option<ir::VariantId> {
                if matches!(builtin, Builtin::Len | Builtin::Count | Builtin::CountCapped | Builtin::Sum) {
                    if let Some((value, steps)) = borrowed(&self.env, &args[0], &self.statement_site)? {
                        self.meter.step(steps)?;
                        return scalar_builtin(builtin, value);
                    }
                }
                let args = args.iter().map(|value| self.expr(value)).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
                self.builtin(builtin, args)
            }
            Expr::Get(read) => {
                let value = self.get(read)?;
                self.meter.heap(expression_heap(&value))?;
                Ok(value)
            }
            Expr::Exists(read) => {
                let value = self.get(read)?;
                // The private null-value aggregate result preserves scalar
                // denial versus authorized zero; it is not an existing row.
                Ok(Value::Bool(value != Value::Null && !(self.schema.maintain_for(&read.table).is_some() && value.field("value").ok() == Some(&Value::Null))))
            }
            Expr::Scan(scan) => {
                let values = self.scan(scan)?;
                self.meter.heap(values.iter().map(expression_heap).sum::<u64>())?;
                Ok(Value::Array(values))
            }
            Expr::Merge(merge) => {
                let values = self.merge(merge, |runner, values| runner.meter.heap(values.iter().map(expression_heap).sum::<u64>()))?;
                Ok(Value::Array(values))
            }
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn input(&mut self, input: Input) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
        match input {
            Input::Viewer => {
                self.meter.heap(self.context.viewer.len() as u64)?;
                Ok(Value::Id(self.context.viewer.clone()))
            }
            Input::Now => self.context.now.map(Value::Int).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("now is not pinned in a query".to_owned())),
            Input::NewId => {
                let id = self.context.mint()?;
                let value = Value::Id(id);
                crate::value::validate_identities(&value)?;
                self.meter.heap(expression_heap(&value))?;
                Ok(value)
            }
            Input::ReadTime => self.context.read_time.map(Value::Int).ok_or_else(|| RuntimeError::Input("readTime is not pinned".to_owned())),
        }
    }

    fn builtin(&mut self, builtin: &Builtin, mut args: Vec<Value>) -> Result<Value, RuntimeError> {
        let result: Result<Value, RuntimeError> = match builtin {
            #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
            Builtin::JobAs | Builtin::JobQuery { .. } => unreachable!("job builtins dispatch before evaluating arguments"),
            Builtin::ExtremumOf { .. } | Builtin::Extremum { .. } => unreachable!("column extrema evaluate their source under scalar coverage"),
            Builtin::ReceiptSettled { table } => {
                // This primitive remains for settled-only query accounting.
                // Raw IR invoking it during prediction must keep queue custody.
                if self.device_allowance.is_some() {
                    return Err(RuntimeError::Prediction("receipt state needs server settlement".into()));
                }
                Ok(Value::Bool(!self.is_provisional(table, &args[0])?))
            },
            Builtin::ProjectFields { table } => {
                let row = args.remove(0);
                if row == Value::Null { Ok(row) } else {
                    let row = self.with_defaults(table, row.expression_input(ExpressionAccess(())))?.into_value();
                    self.project_row(table, row).map(|row| super::recorded::public_image(&self.schema.tables[table], row.into_value()))
                }
            }
            Builtin::OptionalField { field } => {
                #[cfg(feature = "sqlite")]
                self.delivery_field(&args[0], field);
                Ok(args[0].field(field).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null))
            },
            Builtin::Len | Builtin::Count | Builtin::CountCapped | Builtin::Sum => scalar_builtin(builtin, &args[0]),
//! Bind a planned view's source lanes independently, preserving source rules.
use super::*;

impl Binder<'_> {
    pub(super) fn planned_view(&mut self, merge: &ast::MergeExpr, out: &mut setup::Body, position: prefix::Position) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let site = self.site();
        let cursor = merge.cursor.as_ref().map(|value| self.expr(value, out)).transpose()?;
        if let Some((_, ty)) = &cursor && ty.required() != Ty::Cursor {
            return Err(type_error(&merge.site, "after expects a cursor", "declare c: cursor ? and pass after c"));
        }
        let mut lanes = Vec::new();
        let mut result: Option<BTreeMap<String, Ty>> = None;
        let mut first_site = None;
        let mut field_sources: BTreeMap<String, Vec<(Ty, A)>> = BTreeMap::new();
        // Queries retain the view's shared cursor identity. A non-query with
        // one source is an ordinary admitted scan followed by a row shape;
        // representing it as a merge would reject safe capped mutations.
        let plain = self.caller_kind != ProgramKind::Query && merge.lanes.len() == 1
            && merge.lanes[0].project.is_some() && merge.lanes[0].loop_.is_none();
        for lane in &merge.lanes {
            let mut read = *lane.planned.clone().expect("planned lane");
            // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — planning cannot erase point-key admission.
            // Validate the authored predicate in the caller's lexical scope,
            // before binding the proposed lane and its generated selectors.
            if let Some(predicate) = &lane.admission {
                self.clone().row_predicate(&read.table, predicate)?;
            }
            if let Some(range) = &mut read.range { range.cursor = None; }
            self.push_scope();
            let (source, binding) = if let Some((name, domain)) = &lane.loop_ {
                let (source, ty) = self.lane_domain(domain, out)?;
                (Some(source), self.declare(name, ty))
            } else { (None, String::new()) };
            if let Some((_, domain)) = &lane.loop_ {
                self.sources.insert(format!("for/{binding}"), (domain.site().clone(), self.catalog.source.expression(domain)));
            }
            let distinct = lane.distinct.as_ref().map(|value| self.expr(value, out).map(|(value, _)| value)).transpose()?;
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.12 — as with authored merges, a lane-dependent
            // residual needs its own selector in eager setup. Deduplication
            // above still reads the original discovery binding.
            if let Some((name, _)) = &lane.loop_
                && read.filter.as_ref().is_some_and(|filter| lanes::filter_uses_lane(filter, name)) {
                let selector = self.temp("selector");
                self.scopes.last_mut().expect("lane scope").get_mut(name).expect("lane binding").runtime = selector.clone();
                setup.push(Stmt::Let { name: selector, value: ir::var(&binding) });
            }
            let (mut scan, _) = self.scan_at(&read, &mut setup, position)?;
            scan.after = cursor.as_ref().map(|(value, _)| Box::new(value.clone()));
            scan.opaque_cursor = cursor.is_some();
            scan.merge_site = Some(site.clone());
            if lane.branch.as_deref() == Some(ir::BOUNDED_LANE) {
                let proof = MergeLane { guard: None, distinct: None, branch: Some(ir::BOUNDED_LANE.into()), setup: Vec::new(), scan: scan.clone(), source: None, binding: String::new(), project: None };
                if !snapback4_core::engine::rules::bounded_branch(self.schema, &proof) {
                    // The schema's null-exempt caps or lifetime dependencies
                    // could not certify this speculative source-level plan.
                    self.pop_scope();
                    let mut original = read.clone();
                    if let Some(range) = &mut original.range { range.cursor = merge.cursor.clone(); }
                    return self.read(&original, out);
                }
            }
            if lane.project.is_none() {
                // Admission was checked against the original domain cap above.
                scan.take = Bound::Literal(merge.take);
                self.pop_scope();
                self.push_value_lane(&mut lanes, lane, MergeLane { guard: None, distinct, branch: lane.branch.clone(), setup: setup.code, scan, source, binding, project: None }, position)?;
                continue;
            }
            scan.after = None;
            let mut conjuncts = Vec::new();
            if let Some(filter) = &read.filter { super::super::intent::split(*filter.clone(), &mut conjuncts); }
            let guard = super::super::intent::conjunction(conjuncts.into_iter().filter(super::super::intent::independent).collect());
            let guard = guard.map(|guard| self.expr(&guard, out).map(|(value, _)| value)).transpose()?;
            let mut projection_setup = setup::Body::default();
            let project_binding = if plain { self.temp("view_row") } else { "$project".into() };
            let (project, ty) = self.shape_object(&project_binding, Ty::Row(read.table.clone()), lane.project.as_ref().expect("view projection"), &lane.site, &mut projection_setup)?;
            if plain {
                scan.merge_site = None;
                scan.after = cursor.as_ref().map(|(value, _)| Box::new(value.clone()));
                let max = scan.take.maximum() as u32;
                let target = self.temp("view_rows");
                out.extend(setup);
                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: target.clone(), value: Expr::Array(Vec::new()) });
                projection_setup.push(Stmt::Push { target: target.clone(), value: project });
                out.push_for(project_binding, LoopDomain::Page(scan), projection_setup);
                self.pop_scope();
                return Ok((ir::var(&target), Ty::Collection(Box::new(ty), max)));
            }
            let fields = match ty { Ty::RowShape(fields) | Ty::RowObject(fields) | Ty::Object(fields) => fields, _ => unreachable!("shape object") };
            if let Some(prior) = &mut result {
                if prior.keys().ne(fields.keys()) {
                    return Err(type_error(&lane.site, format!("view {} branch fields differ from branch at {}", merge.table, first_site.as_ref().unwrap()), "project the same named fields in every view branch").guide("reads"));
                }
                for (name, ty) in fields {
                    let old = &prior[&name];
                    let current = projection_source(lane, &name);
                    let origins = field_sources.get_mut(&name).unwrap();
                    origins.push((ty.clone(), current));
                    old.join(&ty).map_err(|error| self.join_error_many(error.at(&name), origins))?;
                    let joined = view_join(old, &ty).ok_or_else(|| type_error(&lane.site,
                        format!("view {} field {name}: {} conflicts with {} at {}", merge.table, ty.name(), old.name(), first_site.as_ref().unwrap()),
                        format!("project compatible values for {name} in every branch")).guide("reads"))?;
                    prior.insert(name, joined);
                }
            } else {
                first_site = Some(lane.site.clone());
                field_sources = fields.keys().map(|name| (name.clone(), vec![(fields[name].clone(), projection_source(lane, name))])).collect();
                result = Some(fields);
            }
            let project = if projection_setup.is_empty() { project } else { Expr::Block { body: projection_setup.code, value: Box::new(project) } };
            self.pop_scope();
            self.push_value_lane(&mut lanes, lane, MergeLane { guard, distinct, branch: lane.branch.clone(), setup: setup.code, scan, source, binding, project: Some(project) }, position)?;
        }
        if result.is_none() && !lanes.is_empty() {
            let planned = Merge { variant: self.variant(), position: None, view: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), site, lanes };
            // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#3 — the binding check: the runtime's own analysis must agree that every
            // lane is decided (a grant lane by a grant covering the rule) and that the lanes share one
            // position. Otherwise a page with a grant branch reads as written, the grant branch alone.
            if planned.branches() && !snapback4_core::engine::rules::branch_merge_lazy(self.schema, &planned)
                && let Some(read) = merge.lanes.iter().find(|lane| matches!(lane.branch.as_deref(), Some(ir::GRANT_LANE | ir::SYSTEM_LANE))).and_then(|lane| lane.planned.as_deref()) {
                // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#131-notes-and-declarations — declining a branch merge must preserve the caller's continuation.
                let mut original = read.clone();
                if let Some(range) = &mut original.range { range.cursor = merge.cursor.clone(); }
                return self.read(&original, out);
            }
            return Ok((Expr::Merge(planned), Ty::Page(merge.table.clone(), merge.take)));
        }
        let fields = match self.catalog.view_types.get(&merge.table) {
            Some(Ty::RowShape(fields)) => fields.clone(), _ => result.unwrap_or_default(),
